### Task 4: The terminal host: a PTY the daemon owns, its scrollback and its panes

The `terminal/` module knows nothing of workstreams or the database. It spawns a program in a PTY under a key, keeps the newest output, streams it to every attached pane, takes their keystrokes and sizes, reports how the process ended, and ends every terminal at shutdown. Task 6 keys it by workstream.

**Files:**

- Modify: `apps/daemon/package.json`, `pnpm-lock.yaml` (`@lydell/node-pty` 1.1.0)
- Create: `apps/daemon/src/terminal/pty.ts`, `scrollback.ts`, `host.ts`, `index.ts`
- Create: `apps/daemon/test/fake-claude.ts`
- Test: `apps/daemon/src/terminal/scrollback.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/terminal/host.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 1's `TerminalSize`, `TERMINAL_SIZE_LIMITS`; the existing `Clock`, `Timer`, `ManualClock`, `Logger`, `silentLogger`, `tempDir`, `waitFor`.
- Produces (from `terminal/index.ts`):
  - `PtyProcess` `{ pid; onData(listener); onExit(listener: (exit: { exitCode: number; signal: number | null }) => void); write(data); resize(size: TerminalSize); kill(signal: 'SIGHUP' | 'SIGKILL') }`, `PtySpawner = (file, args, options: { cwd; env; size }) => PtyProcess`, `nodePtySpawner`.
  - `Scrollback` `{ append(data); text(); clear() }`, `createScrollback(limit: number): Scrollback`.
  - `DEFAULT_TERMINAL_LIMITS = { scrollbackChars: 262_144, stopGraceMs: 3_000 }`; inside `host.ts`, `DEFAULT_TERMINAL_SIZE = { cols: 120, rows: 32 }`, the size a terminal spawns at before a pane sends its own.
  - `TerminalSpec` `{ file; args: readonly string[]; cwd; env: Readonly<Record<string, string>>; banner: readonly string[]; onFirstOutput?: () => void }`, `TerminalEnd` `{ exitCode: number | null; signal: number | null; stopped: boolean }`, `TerminalViewer` `{ output(data); ended(end: TerminalEnd); close() }`, `TerminalAttachment` `{ input(data); resize(size: TerminalSize); detach() }`, `TerminalSpawnError`.
  - `TerminalHost { open(key, spec, onEnd): { pid: number }; attach(key, viewer): TerminalAttachment | null; isRunning(key): boolean; forget(key): void; stopAll(): Promise<void> }`. Once `stopAll` has begun, `open` throws `TerminalSpawnError('10db is shutting down')`. `stopAll` sends SIGHUP to each program, then, after the grace, SIGKILL to its whole process group (forkpty makes the program a session and group leader, so its own children are in the group), `createTerminalHost(opts: { spawner?: PtySpawner; clock: Clock; logger: Logger; limits?: TerminalLimits }): TerminalHost`.
  - Test helper `createFakeClaude(opts?: { ignoreHangup?: boolean; silent?: boolean }): FakeClaude` (`test/fake-claude.ts`): `{ path; lastRun(): { args: string[]; cwd: string; env: Map<string, string> } | null; cleanup() }`. The script records its arguments, directory and environment, prints `fake claude: ready` (unless `silent`), then answers each line it reads: `/exit` exits 0 after `fake claude: bye`, `size` prints `stty size` (`<rows> <cols>`), `flood <n>` prints n numbered lines, anything else prints `you said: <line>`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Write the fake `claude`**

`apps/daemon/test/fake-claude.ts`:

```ts
import { chmodSync, existsSync, mkdirSync, readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { tempDir } from './fixtures';

/**
 * Stands in for `claude` at the child-process boundary: the PTY, the arguments, the directory and the
 * environment are real. It records how it was run, then answers each line it reads.
 */
export interface FakeClaude {
  readonly path: string;
  /** Its newest run's arguments, directory and environment, once it has started. */
  lastRun(): { args: string[]; cwd: string; env: Map<string, string> } | null;
  cleanup(): void;
}

const quote = (value: string): string => `'${value.replaceAll("'", `'\\''`)}'`;

export function createFakeClaude(
  opts: { readonly ignoreHangup?: boolean; readonly silent?: boolean } = {},
): FakeClaude {
  const dir = tempDir('fake-claude');
  const record = join(dir.path, 'record');
  mkdirSync(record);
  const path = join(dir.path, 'claude');
  writeFileSync(
    path,
    [
      '#!/bin/sh',
      `printf '%s\\n' "$@" > ${quote(join(record, 'args'))}`,
      `pwd -P > ${quote(join(record, 'cwd'))}`,
      `env > ${quote(join(record, 'env'))}`,
      // A process that ignores SIGHUP, as a busy program might, with a child of its own: the host
      // must SIGKILL the whole group.
      ...(opts.ignoreHangup ? ["trap '' HUP", 'sleep 600 &', 'echo "fake claude: child $!"'] : []),
      // A program that prints nothing until it is spoken to.
      ...(opts.silent ? [] : ['echo "fake claude: ready"']),
      'while IFS= read -r line; do',
      '  case "$line" in',
      '    /exit) echo "fake claude: bye"; exit 0 ;;',
      '    size) stty size ;;',
      '    "flood "*) i=0; n=${line#flood }; while [ "$i" -lt "$n" ]; do echo "line $i of the flood"; i=$((i + 1)); done ;;',
      '    *) echo "you said: $line" ;;',
      '  esac',
      'done',
      '',
    ].join('\n'),
  );
  chmodSync(path, 0o755);
  return {
    path,
    lastRun() {
      if (!existsSync(join(record, 'env'))) return null;
      const read = (name: string) => readFileSync(join(record, name), 'utf8');
      return {
        args: read('args').split('\n').filter(Boolean),
        cwd: read('cwd').trim(),
        env: new Map(
          read('env')
            .split('\n')
            .filter((line) => line.includes('='))
            .map((line) => [line.slice(0, line.indexOf('=')), line.slice(line.indexOf('=') + 1)]),
        ),
      };
    },
    cleanup() {
      dir.cleanup();
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
  it.each([
    { name: 'keeps everything under the limit', chunks: ['ab', 'cd'], limit: 10, text: 'abcd' },
    { name: 'drops whole old chunks first', chunks: ['abc', 'def', 'gh'], limit: 5, text: 'defgh' },
```

`apps/daemon/src/terminal/host.test.ts`:

```ts
import { mkdirSync, realpathSync } from 'node:fs';
import { join } from 'node:path';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createFakeClaude, type FakeClaude } from '../../test/fake-claude';
import { tempDir, waitFor, type TempDir } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { ManualClock } from '../clock';
import { silentLogger } from '../log';
import {
  createTerminalHost,
  DEFAULT_TERMINAL_LIMITS,
  TerminalSpawnError,
  type TerminalEnd,
  type TerminalHost,
  type TerminalSpec,
} from './index';

const LIMITS = { scrollbackChars: 4_096, stopGraceMs: DEFAULT_TERMINAL_LIMITS.stopGraceMs };

let dir: TempDir;
let claude: FakeClaude;
let clock: ManualClock;
let host: TerminalHost;
beforeEach(() => {
  dir = tempDir('terminal-host');
  mkdirSync(join(dir.path, 'repo'));
  claude = createFakeClaude();
  clock = new ManualClock();
  host = createTerminalHost({ clock, logger: silentLogger, limits: LIMITS });
});
afterEach(async () => {
  const stopping = host.stopAll();
  clock.advance(LIMITS.stopGraceMs);
  await stopping;
  claude.cleanup();
  dir.cleanup();
});

const spec = (overrides: Partial<TerminalSpec> = {}): TerminalSpec => ({
  file: claude.path,
  args: ['--resume', 'provider-1'],
  cwd: join(dir.path, 'repo'),
  env: { PATH: '/usr/bin:/bin', HOME: dir.path, MARK: 'from the spec' },
  banner: ['10db: hello'],
  ...overrides,
});

/** A pane that records what it is sent. */
function pane() {
  const seen = { text: '', ends: [] as TerminalEnd[], closed: false };
  return {
    seen,
    viewer: {
      output: (data: string) => {
        seen.text += data;
      },
      ended: (end: TerminalEnd) => {
        seen.ends.push(end);
      },
      close: () => {
        seen.closed = true;
      },
    },
  };
}

function opened(key = 'ws_1', overrides: Partial<TerminalSpec> = {}) {
  const ends: TerminalEnd[] = [];
  const { pid } = host.open(key, spec(overrides), (end) => {
    ends.push(end);
  });
  return { pid, ends };
}

describe('the terminal host', () => {
  it('runs the program in a PTY in its directory and env, banner first', async () => {
    let printed = 0;
    const { pid } = opened('ws_1', {
      onFirstOutput: () => {
        printed += 1;
      },
    });
    const a = pane();
    const attached = host.attach('ws_1', a.viewer);
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('fake claude: ready'));
    attached?.input('more\r');
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('you said: more'));
    // The banner isn't the program's output; the first thing the program prints is, once.
    expect(printed).toBe(1);
    expect(pid).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(a.seen.text.startsWith('10db: hello\r\n')).toBe(true);
    expect(claude.lastRun()).toMatchObject({
      args: ['--resume', 'provider-1'],
      cwd: realpathSync(join(dir.path, 'repo')),
    });
    expect(claude.lastRun()?.env.get('MARK')).toBe('from the spec');
    expect(host.isRunning('ws_1')).toBe(true);
  });

  it('sends keystrokes to the program and its answer back', async () => {
    opened();
    const a = pane();
    const attached = host.attach('ws_1', a.viewer);
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('ready'));
    attached?.input('hello\r');
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('you said: hello'));
  });

  it('resizes the PTY', async () => {
    opened();
    const a = pane();
    const attached = host.attach('ws_1', a.viewer);
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('ready'));
    attached?.resize({ cols: 100, rows: 40 });
    attached?.input('size\r');
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('40 100'));
  });

  it('streams one terminal to two panes, and either types into it', async () => {
    opened();
    const a = pane();
    const b = pane();
    const fromA = host.attach('ws_1', a.viewer);
    const fromB = host.attach('ws_1', b.viewer);
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('ready') && b.seen.text.includes('ready'));
    fromA?.input('one\r');
    fromB?.input('two\r');
    await waitFor(() => b.seen.text.includes('you said: one') && a.seen.text.includes('you said: two'));
  });

  it('gives a pane that reattaches the recent output, then live output', async () => {
    opened();
    const a = pane();
    const first = host.attach('ws_1', a.viewer);
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('ready'));
    first?.input('one\r');
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('you said: one'));
    first?.detach();
    // Nobody watches; the process runs on.
    const b = pane();
    const second = host.attach('ws_1', b.viewer);
    expect(b.seen.text).toContain('you said: one');
    second?.input('two\r');
    await waitFor(() => b.seen.text.includes('you said: two'));
    expect(a.seen.text).not.toContain('you said: two');
  });

  it('keeps only the newest output for a pane that attaches late', async () => {
    opened();
    const a = pane();
    const watching = host.attach('ws_1', a.viewer);
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('ready'));
    watching?.input('flood 400\r');
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('line 399 of the flood'));
    const late = pane();
    host.attach('ws_1', late.viewer);
    expect(late.seen.text.length).toBeLessThanOrEqual(LIMITS.scrollbackChars);
    expect(late.seen.text).toContain('line 399 of the flood');
    expect(late.seen.text).not.toContain('line 0 of the flood');
  });

  it('reports how the program ended, to its owner and to every pane, a late one too', async () => {
    const { ends } = opened();
    const a = pane();
    const attached = host.attach('ws_1', a.viewer);
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('ready'));
    attached?.input('/exit\r');
    await waitFor(() => ends.length === 1);
    const exited: TerminalEnd = { exitCode: 0, signal: null, stopped: false };
    expect(ends).toEqual([exited]);
    expect(a.seen.ends).toEqual([exited]);
    expect(a.seen.text).toContain('10db: Claude exited with status 0.');
    expect(host.isRunning('ws_1')).toBe(false);
    attached?.input('ignored\r');
    const late = pane();
    host.attach('ws_1', late.viewer);
    expect(late.seen.ends).toEqual([exited]);
    expect(late.seen.text).toContain('fake claude: bye');
  });

  it('ends every terminal at stopAll: SIGHUP, then SIGKILL after the grace', async () => {
    const stubborn = createFakeClaude({ ignoreHangup: true });
    try {
      const polite = opened('ws_1');
      const deaf = opened('ws_2', { file: stubborn.path });
      const a = pane();
      const b = pane();
      host.attach('ws_1', a.viewer);
      host.attach('ws_2', b.viewer);
      await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('ready') && b.seen.text.includes('ready'));
      const grandchild = Number(/fake claude: child (\d+)/.exec(b.seen.text)?.[1]);

      const stopping = host.stopAll();
      expect(() => opened('ws_3')).toThrow(new TerminalSpawnError('10db is shutting down'));
      await waitFor(() => polite.ends.length === 1);
      expect(polite.ends[0]).toMatchObject({ stopped: true });
      expect(host.isRunning('ws_2')).toBe(true);
      clock.advance(LIMITS.stopGraceMs);
      await stopping;
      expect(deaf.ends).toEqual([{ exitCode: null, signal: 9, stopped: true }]);
      // The SIGKILL went to the program's group, so the child it started is gone too.
      await waitFor(() => {
        try {
          process.kill(grandchild, 0);
          return undefined;
        } catch {
          return true;
        }
      });
    } finally {
      stubborn.cleanup();
    }
  });

  it('refuses a second terminal for a key while one runs, and opens a new one once it ended', async () => {
    const { ends } = opened();
    expect(() => opened()).toThrow(TerminalSpawnError);
    const a = pane();
    const attached = host.attach('ws_1', a.viewer);
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('ready'));
    attached?.input('/exit\r');
    await waitFor(() => ends.length === 1);
    opened();
    await waitFor(() => (a.seen.text.match(/fake claude: ready/g) ?? []).length === 2);
  });

  it('wraps a PTY that cannot spawn in a TerminalSpawnError', () => {
    const broken = createTerminalHost({
      // The PTY library itself is the boundary here: this is how it reports a broken spawn-helper.
      spawner: () => {
        throw new Error('posix_spawnp failed.');
      },
      clock,
      logger: silentLogger,
    });
    expect(() => broken.open('ws_1', spec(), () => undefined)).toThrow(
      new TerminalSpawnError('posix_spawnp failed.'),
    );
    expect(broken.attach('ws_1', pane().viewer)).toBeNull();
  });

  it('forgets an ended terminal and closes its panes, and has nothing for an unknown key', async () => {
    const { ends } = opened();
    const a = pane();
    const attached = host.attach('ws_1', a.viewer);
    await waitFor(() => a.seen.text.includes('ready'));
    attached?.input('/exit\r');
    await waitFor(() => ends.length === 1);
    host.forget('ws_1');
    expect(a.seen.closed).toBe(true);
    expect(host.attach('ws_1', pane().viewer)).toBeNull();
    expect(host.attach('ws_unknown', pane().viewer)).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the PTY boundary and the scrollback**

`apps/daemon/src/terminal/pty.ts`:

```ts
import { spawn } from '@lydell/node-pty';
import type { TerminalSize } from '@10db/contracts';

/** A program in a pseudo-terminal: the one boundary `terminal/` drives. */
export interface PtyProcess {
  readonly pid: number;
  onData(listener: (data: string) => void): void;
  onExit(listener: (exit: { readonly exitCode: number; readonly signal: number | null }) => void): void;
  write(data: string): void;
  resize(size: TerminalSize): void;
  /** SIGHUP the program; SIGKILL its whole process group. */
  kill(signal: 'SIGHUP' | 'SIGKILL'): void;
}

export type PtySpawner = (
  file: string,
  args: readonly string[],
  options: {
    readonly cwd: string;
    readonly env: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
    readonly size: TerminalSize;
  },
) => PtyProcess;

/** Spawns through @lydell/node-pty, which nothing else in the daemon imports. */
export const nodePtySpawner: PtySpawner = (file, args, options) => {
  const pty = spawn(file, [...args], {
    name: 'xterm-256color',
    cwd: options.cwd,
    env: { ...options.env },
    cols: options.size.cols,
    rows: options.size.rows,
  });
  return {
    pid: pty.pid,
    onData(listener) {
      pty.onData(listener);
    },
    onExit(listener) {
      // node-pty reports signal 0 for a normal exit.
      pty.onExit(({ exitCode, signal }) => {
        listener({ exitCode, signal: signal ? signal : null });
      });
    },
    write(data) {
      pty.write(data);
    },
    resize({ cols, rows }) {
      pty.resize(cols, rows);
    },
    kill(signal) {
      // forkpty makes the program a session and group leader, so the children it started share its
      // group; a SIGKILL at shutdown ends them too. SIGHUP goes to the program, as a hangup does.
      if (signal === 'SIGKILL') {
        try {
          process.kill(-pty.pid, 'SIGKILL');
          return;
        } catch {
          // The group is gone already; fall through to the program itself.
        }
      }
      pty.kill(signal);
    },
  };
};
```

`apps/daemon/src/terminal/scrollback.ts`:

```ts
/** The newest output of one terminal, at most `limit` UTF-16 code units, for a pane that attaches late. */
export interface Scrollback {
  append(data: string): void;
  text(): string;
  clear(): void;
}

/**
 * Trims from the front, so the first kept line may start mid-line or mid-escape-sequence; xterm.js
 * shows that as a stray character or two, and everything after it is intact.
 */
export function createScrollback(limit: number): Scrollback {
  let chunks: string[] = [];
  let size = 0;
  return {
    append(data) {
      chunks.push(data);
      size += data.length;
      while (size > limit) {
        const [oldest, ...rest] = chunks;
        if (oldest === undefined) break;
        const over = size - limit;
        if (oldest.length <= over) {
          chunks = rest;
          size -= oldest.length;
        } else {
          chunks = [oldest.slice(over), ...rest];
          size -= over;
        }
      }
    },
    text: () => chunks.join(''),
    clear() {
      chunks = [];
      size = 0;
    },
  };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Write the host**

`apps/daemon/src/terminal/host.ts`:

```ts
import type { TerminalSize } from '@10db/contracts';
import type { Clock } from '../clock';
import type { Logger } from '../log';
import { nodePtySpawner, type PtyProcess, type PtySpawner } from './pty';
import { createScrollback, type Scrollback } from './scrollback';

export const DEFAULT_TERMINAL_LIMITS = {
  /** Output kept per terminal for a pane that attaches late, in UTF-16 code units. */
  scrollbackChars: 262_144,
  /** How long a terminal gets to exit after SIGHUP at shutdown before SIGKILL. */
  stopGraceMs: 3_000,
} as const;
export type TerminalLimits = { readonly scrollbackChars: number; readonly stopGraceMs: number };

/** A terminal's size until a pane attaches and sends its own. */
export const DEFAULT_TERMINAL_SIZE: TerminalSize = { cols: 120, rows: 32 };

export interface TerminalSpec {
  readonly file: string;
  readonly args: readonly string[];
  readonly cwd: string;
  readonly env: Readonly<Record<string, string>>;
  /** Lines shown above the program's own output. */
  readonly banner: readonly string[];
  /** Runs once, when the program first prints; the banner doesn't count. */
  readonly onFirstOutput?: () => void;
}

/** How a terminal's process ended; `stopped` when the host ended it at shutdown. */
export interface TerminalEnd {
  /** The exit status; null when a signal ended it. */
  readonly exitCode: number | null;
  readonly signal: number | null;
  readonly stopped: boolean;
}

/** A pane: it is sent output and the end, and closed when the terminal is forgotten. */
export interface TerminalViewer {
  output(data: string): void;
  ended(end: TerminalEnd): void;
  close(): void;
}

export interface TerminalAttachment {
  /** Keystrokes for the process; dropped once it has ended. */
  input(data: string): void;
  resize(size: TerminalSize): void;
  detach(): void;
}

export class TerminalSpawnError extends Error {
  override readonly name = 'TerminalSpawnError';
}

export interface TerminalHost {
  /**
   * Spawns `spec` under `key`, which must have no running terminal; a pane already attached to the
   * key's ended terminal stays attached. `onEnd` runs once when the process ends. Throws
   * TerminalSpawnError when the PTY can't spawn it.
   */
  open(
    key: string,
    spec: TerminalSpec,
    onEnd: (end: TerminalEnd) => void,
  ): { readonly pid: number };
  /** Sends `key`'s kept output, then its live output, to `viewer`; null when the host has no terminal for it. */
  attach(key: string, viewer: TerminalViewer): TerminalAttachment | null;
  isRunning(key: string): boolean;
  /** Drops an ended terminal's output and closes its panes; a running one is left alone. */
  forget(key: string): void;
  /**
   * Refuses new terminals, sends SIGHUP to every running one and, after the grace, SIGKILL to its
   * process group; resolves once all have ended.
   */
  stopAll(): Promise<void>;
}

interface Slot {
  process: PtyProcess | null;
  readonly scrollback: Scrollback;
  readonly viewers: Set<TerminalViewer>;
  lastEnd: TerminalEnd | null;
  stopping: boolean;
  ended: Promise<void>;
}

function exitLine(end: TerminalEnd): string {
  if (end.stopped) return '\r\n10db: 10db stopped, so this terminal ended. Open it again to resume.\r\n';
  const how =
    end.exitCode === null ? `was ended by signal ${String(end.signal)}` : `exited with status ${String(end.exitCode)}`;
  return `\r\n10db: Claude ${how}. Press Hand back in 10db when you are done.\r\n`;
}

/** The daemon's PTYs, one per key (spec §5.4: the terminal owns the session while it is taken over). */
export function createTerminalHost(opts: {
  readonly spawner?: PtySpawner;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly logger: Logger;
  readonly limits?: TerminalLimits;
}): TerminalHost {
  const spawner = opts.spawner ?? nodePtySpawner;
  const limits = opts.limits ?? DEFAULT_TERMINAL_LIMITS;
  const slots = new Map<string, Slot>();
  /** Set once stopAll begins: a terminal opened after it would outlive the shutdown. */
  let shuttingDown = false;

  function show(slot: Slot, data: string): void {
    slot.scrollback.append(data);
    for (const viewer of slot.viewers) viewer.output(data);
  }

  return {
    open(key, spec, onEnd) {
      if (shuttingDown) throw new TerminalSpawnError('10db is shutting down');
      const existing = slots.get(key);
      if (existing?.process) throw new TerminalSpawnError(`A terminal already runs for ${key}`);
      let child: PtyProcess;
      try {
        child = spawner(spec.file, spec.args, {
          cwd: spec.cwd,
          env: spec.env,
          size: DEFAULT_TERMINAL_SIZE,
        });
      } catch (error) {
        throw new TerminalSpawnError(error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error));
      }
      let finished: () => void = () => undefined;
      const slot: Slot = existing ?? {
        process: null,
        scrollback: createScrollback(limits.scrollbackChars),
        viewers: new Set(),
        lastEnd: null,
        stopping: false,
        ended: Promise.resolve(),
      };
      slot.scrollback.clear();
      slot.process = child;
      slot.lastEnd = null;
      slot.stopping = false;
      slot.ended = new Promise<void>((resolve) => {
        finished = resolve;
      });
      slots.set(key, slot);
      show(slot, spec.banner.map((line) => `${line}\r\n`).join(''));
      let printed = false;
      child.onData((data) => {
        if (!printed) {
          printed = true;
          spec.onFirstOutput?.();
        }
        show(slot, data);
      });
      child.onExit(({ exitCode, signal }) => {
        const end: TerminalEnd = {
          exitCode: signal === null ? exitCode : null,
          signal,
          stopped: slot.stopping,
        };
        slot.process = null;
        slot.lastEnd = end;
        show(slot, exitLine(end));
        for (const viewer of slot.viewers) viewer.ended(end);
        try {
          onEnd(end);
        } catch (error) {
          opts.logger.error({ key, err: String(error) }, 'recording a terminal end failed');
        }
        finished();
      });
      return { pid: child.pid };
    },
    attach(key, viewer) {
      const slot = slots.get(key);
      if (!slot) return null;
      const kept = slot.scrollback.text();
      if (kept) viewer.output(kept);
      if (slot.lastEnd) viewer.ended(slot.lastEnd);
      slot.viewers.add(viewer);
      return {
        input(data) {
          slot.process?.write(data);
        },
        resize(size) {
          slot.process?.resize(size);
        },
        detach() {
          slot.viewers.delete(viewer);
        },
      };
    },
    isRunning: (key) => (slots.get(key)?.process ?? null) !== null,
    forget(key) {
      const slot = slots.get(key);
      if (!slot || slot.process) return;
      for (const viewer of slot.viewers) viewer.close();
      slots.delete(key);
    },
    async stopAll() {
      shuttingDown = true;
      const running = [...slots.values()].filter((slot) => slot.process);
      for (const slot of running) {
        slot.stopping = true;
        slot.process?.kill('SIGHUP');
      }
      const kill = opts.clock.setTimeout(() => {
        for (const slot of running) slot.process?.kill('SIGKILL');
      }, limits.stopGraceMs);
      await Promise.all(running.map((slot) => slot.ended));
      kill.cancel();
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
} from './host';
```

### Task 5: The runner pauses a session at a safe point and enters `TAKEN_OVER`

**Files:**

- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/runner/index.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/briefing/prompts.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/briefing/index.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/inbox/service.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/inbox/index.ts`
- Test: `apps/daemon/src/runner/runner.test.ts` (a new `describe('takeover')`), `apps/daemon/src/inbox/inbox.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 2's `enterTakeover`, `latestConversation`, `latestEventSeq`; the batch plan's `Git.currentBranch`.
- Produces:
  - `StopReason` gains `'takeover'`. `settle`'s `stopped` case becomes an exhaustive switch over `StopReason`, with an arm for `takeover` that `run()` never reaches.
  - `RunnerLimits.takeoverWaitMs: number`; `DEFAULT_RUNNER_LIMITS.takeoverWaitMs = 60_000`.
  - `Runner.takeOver(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<void>`. It resolves once the runner has let go of the workstream. The workstream is then `TAKEN_OVER`, unless something else ended the session first (a cancel, a shutdown, a usage limit, a crash), and the caller reads which. It resolves at once, leaving the run alone, when the takeover lapses: while the gate runs (`VERIFYING` or `nextAction` `verify`), or when the session stopped before it began a conversation (the step then runs on, with no recovery attempt spent). It does nothing when no runner holds the workstream, and rejects with `ConflictError` when the runner is already stopping for another reason.
  - `TAKEOVER_REFUSAL` (runner) and `TAKEOVER_NOTICE` (briefing): the guard's denial and the message sent to the session. Both contain "taking this session over".
  - `cancelWorkstreamCards(ctx: WriteContext, workstreamId: WorkstreamId, kinds?: readonly InboxKind[]): void` (`inbox/`): cancels the workstream's open cards inside the caller's write, so a takeover cancels them in the same transaction as its transition; given `kinds`, only cards of those kinds. `InboxService.cancelForWorkstream` keeps its signature and calls it.

- [ ] **Step 3: Cancel cards inside the caller's write**

In `apps/daemon/src/inbox/service.ts` (import `type InboxKind` from `@10db/contracts` and `type WriteContext` from `@10db/db` where missing), move the body of `cancelForWorkstream`'s write into an exported function, and add the `kinds` filter:

```ts
/**
 * Cancels the workstream's open cards inside the caller's write; `kinds` narrows it. A card folded
 * with other workstreams' questions only loses this workstream as a target. A takeover calls it in the
 * same write as its transition, so no answer lands on a card the terminal will ask again.
 */
export function cancelWorkstreamCards(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  workstreamId: WorkstreamId,
  kinds?: readonly InboxKind[],
): void {
  for (const item of openItemsForWorkstream(ctx.tx, workstreamId)) {
    if (item.state !== InboxState.Open) continue;
    if (kinds && !kinds.includes(item.kind)) continue;
    const targets = item.targets.filter((t) => t.workstreamId !== workstreamId);
    updateInboxItem(
      ctx,
      item.id,
      targets.length === 0 ? { state: InboxState.Cancelled, targets } : { targets },
    );
  }
}
```

`cancelForWorkstream(workstreamId)` becomes `deps.state.write((ctx) => { cancelWorkstreamCards(ctx, workstreamId); });`. In `apps/daemon/src/inbox/index.ts`, add `cancelWorkstreamCards` to the `./service` export.

6. Right after `ctl.handle = handle;`, add:

```ts
// The safe point (spec §5.4): at once while the session waits on the builder; otherwise the next tool
// call (denied above) or the end of the turn, and at the latest after takeoverWaitMs.
const safePoint = (): void => {
  if (openWaits() > 0) {
    endWaits();
    void handle?.stop();
    return;
  }
  handle?.send(TAKEOVER_NOTICE);
  st.takeoverTimer ??= opts.clock.setTimeout(() => {
    void handle?.stop();
  }, opts.limits.takeoverWaitMs);
};
ctl.onTakeover = safePoint;
// A takeover asked for while this session was being set up.
if (stopRequested(ctl) === 'takeover') safePoint();
```

10. Add the functions that enter `TAKEN_OVER` or let the takeover lapse, after `finishSession`:

```ts
/**
 * A takeover with nothing to take over lapses (spec §5.4): verification runs no session, and a session
 * stopped before it began a conversation left none to open. The run carries on, no recovery attempt is
 * spent, and the caller hears at once.
 */
function lapseTakeover(ctl: Control): void {
  ctl.stopReason = null;
  ctl.takeoverLapsed?.();
  ctl.takeoverLapsed = null;
}

/**
 * The session stopped at its safe point: the terminal owns the conversation now (spec §5.4). False when
 * the workstream was not taken over: the takeover lapsed, or a cancel, pause or shutdown overtook it.
 */
async function takeOverNow(id: WorkstreamId, ctl: Control): Promise<boolean> {
  const row = getWorkstreamRow(db, id);
  const conversation = latestConversation(db, id);
  if (
    conversation === null ||
    row.nextAction.kind === 'verify' ||
    !canTransition(row.state, S.TakenOver)
  ) {
    lapseTakeover(ctl);
    return false;
  }
  const project = getProject(db, row.projectId);
  const head = await opts.git.head(project.repoPath);
  const branch = await opts.git.currentBranch(project.repoPath);
  // A cancel, pause or shutdown that landed while git was read wins; the caller settles it.
  if (stopRequested(ctl) !== 'takeover') return false;
  opts.state.write((ctx) => {
    // The terminal session asks its own questions. The cards this session waited on are cancelled in
    // the same write as the transition, so no answer lands on one in between.
    cancelWorkstreamCards(ctx, id, [InboxKind.Question, InboxKind.Permission]);
    enterTakeover(ctx, id, ctl.fence, {
      ...conversation,
      head,
      branch,
      takenAt: ctx.now,
      eventSeq: latestEventSeq(ctx.tx),
      rescued: false,
      freshFrom: null,
      lastExit: null,
    });
  });
  return true;
}
```

No path reaches this check with a takeover today. `Runner.takeOver` returns at once while verifying, `launch` runs the first loop top in its own tick, and no await separates the check after `runSession` from the next loop top. It is there so that a takeover always goes before the other stop reasons, and it has no red test of its own.

and right after `const outcome = await runSession(id, ctl);`, before `if (outcome.kind === 'handoff') continue;`:

```ts
  if (outcome.kind === 'stopped') continue;
}
```

A usage limit, a crash or a billing refusal during the wait falls through to `settle` as before.

### Task 6: The takeover service opens the conversation in a daemon terminal

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/daemon/src/takeover/prompt.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/takeover/service.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/takeover/index.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/briefing/prompts.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/briefing/index.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/accounts/cards.ts`
- Create: `apps/daemon/test/takeover-harness.ts`
- Test: `apps/daemon/src/takeover/service.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/briefing/prompts.test.ts`, the accounts plan's cap-card test (`apps/daemon/src/accounts/runtime.test.ts`)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 2's `enterTakeover`, `setSessionProcess`, `latestConversation`, `latestEventSeq`, `WorkstreamDto.takeover`; Task 4's `TerminalHost`, `TerminalSpawnError`, `TerminalViewer`, `TerminalAttachment`, `createFakeClaude`; Task 5's `Runner.takeOver` and `cancelWorkstreamCards`; `mintSessionToken` (`../mcp`); `ProcessTerminator.identify` (`../recovery`); the batch plan's `Git.currentBranch`; the accounts plan's `insertSession` with `accountId`, `getSessionByProviderId`, `patchProject` (a move with work in flight), `Launcher.session`, `AccountLaunchError`, `SessionLaunch`, `unready`, `AccountRuntime.blocked`, `StartGate`, `capCard`, `getAccountRow`, `TestEngine.accounts`, `TestEngine.keychain`, `TestEngine.startGate`; `latestHandoffForStep`.
- Produces:
  - `takeover/prompt.ts`: `TERMINAL_SYSTEM_PROMPT` (the note `--append-system-prompt` carries), `TerminalConversation` (`{ kind: 'resume'; providerSessionId } | { kind: 'fresh'; sessionId; briefing }`), `terminalArgs(input: { conversation: TerminalConversation; model: string; effort: Effort }): string[]`, `takeoverBanner(input: { taskTitle; workstreamId; areas: readonly string[]; accountLabel: string; earlierAccountLabel: string | null }): string[]`.
  - `briefing/`: `terminalBriefing(input: { task; claims; handoff: HandoffDto | null }): string`, the first prompt of a fresh terminal conversation: the task, its claims, and the continuation a `continue_from_handoff` prompt carries (the step's latest handoff, else "inspect git status first").
  - `takeover/service.ts`:
    - `TERMINAL_START_GATE_MS = 30_000`, `TakeoverDeps` (below), `Takeover { takeOver(id): Promise<WorkstreamDto>; attach(id: WorkstreamId, sink: TerminalSink): TerminalControl | null }`, `createTakeover(deps: TakeoverDeps): Takeover`. Task 8 adds `handBack` and `cancel`.
    - `TerminalSink` `{ output(data: string): void; exited(exit: TerminalExit): void; close(): void }` and `TerminalControl` `{ input(data: string): void; resize(size: TerminalSize): void; detach(): void }`: what a pane's socket gives and gets (Task 11's API layer declares the same shapes structurally).
  - Errors: `ConflictError` (409) for a state that can't be taken over, a gate that is running, another workstream in the project taken over or being taken over (T11), a session not started yet, a terminal already running, a session that ended some other way first or whose takeover lapsed, and an account that can't start a session (archived, unready, paused or at its cap, or a launch error); `InvalidError` (400) for a missing or unrunnable `claude`. A PTY that can't spawn is not an error: the takeover stands, `takeover.lastExit` says why and `terminal` is `closed`.
  - The terminal always runs on the project's current account. Opening writes the terminal session, `currentSessionId` and `lastExit: null` in one write that re-reads the workstream (`stillTakenOver`, which Task 8's hand-back and cancel use too). When the record's conversation ran on that account, the terminal resumes it (`--resume`), and its session row copies `briefed` and `briefingBaselinePct` from the session it continues. When it ran on an account the project has since left, the same write switches the record to a fresh conversation, a new UUID on the project's account with `freshFrom` naming the old one, and the terminal starts it with `--session-id <uuid>` and `terminalBriefing` as its first prompt (`briefed` when the briefing carries a handoff). After the write, a terminal on a subscription account takes the account's start gate until Claude first prints, exits or `TERMINAL_START_GATE_MS` passes, then spawns. Once a fresh conversation's terminal has spawned, `freshFrom` is cleared. The pid and its start time are recorded once `identify` answers. A closed terminal (exited, or never spawned) ends its session, clears the workstream's `currentSessionId` and records `lastExit`; closing twice records once.
  - The accounts plan's monthly-cap card says a takeover terminal's spend isn't counted.
  - Test helper `createTakeoverHarness(book, opts?): Promise<TakeoverHarness>` (`test/takeover-harness.ts`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test harness**

`apps/daemon/test/takeover-harness.ts`:

```ts
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import { mkdirSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname, join } from 'node:path';
import type { ProjectInput, TerminalStatus, WorkstreamDto, WorkstreamId } from '@10db/contracts';
import { getWorkstream, latestConversation } from '@10db/db';
import { createLauncher, type Launcher } from '../src/environment';
import { createGit } from '../src/git';
import { silentLogger } from '../src/log';
import { systemProcesses } from '../src/recovery';
import type { FakeScriptBook } from '../src/sessions';
import { createTakeover, type Takeover } from '../src/takeover';
import { createTerminalHost, type TerminalEnd, type TerminalHost } from '../src/terminal';
import { createWatcher, type Watcher } from '../src/watcher';
import { createTestEngine, type TestEngine } from './engine';
import { createFakeClaude, type FakeClaude } from './fake-claude';
import { tempDir, waitFor } from './fixtures';

/** What a builder's login shell might export: billing switches no terminal may inherit. */
export const LEAKY_LOGIN: Readonly<Record<string, string>> = {
  ANTHROPIC_API_KEY: 'sk-from-the-login-shell',
  CLAUDE_CODE_USE_BEDROCK: '1',
  CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR: '/elsewhere/.claude',
};

/** A pane on a workstream's terminal that records what it is sent. */
export interface Pane {
  text(): string;
  readonly ends: TerminalEnd[];
  input(data: string): void;
  detach(): void;
}

export interface TakeoverHarness {
  readonly engine: TestEngine;
  readonly claude: FakeClaude;
  readonly terminals: TerminalHost;
  readonly launcher: Launcher;
  readonly takeover: Takeover;
  readonly watcher: Watcher;
  /** Runs git in the repo, as the builder does in the terminal. */
  git$(...args: string[]): string;
  /** Writes and commits a file in the repo, as the builder does in the terminal; returns the SHA. */
  commitInTerminal(path: string): string;
  /** Waits until the workstream runs a session that has started a conversation. */
  running(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<void>;
  /** Waits until the workstream's terminal is `status` (and, when open, its pid is recorded). */
  terminalIs(id: WorkstreamId, status: TerminalStatus): Promise<WorkstreamDto>;
  /** Attaches a recording pane to the workstream's terminal; throws when there is none. */
  watch(id: WorkstreamId): Pane;
  /** Types a line into the terminal and waits for the fake claude's answer. */
  type(id: WorkstreamId, line: string): Promise<void>;
  /** A new terminal host and takeover service, as after a daemon restart; the database is the same. */
  restartTerminals(): Takeover;
  /** Quits Claude with /exit and waits for the terminal to close. */
  quit(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<WorkstreamDto>;
  close(): Promise<void>;
}

export async function createTakeoverHarness(
  book: FakeScriptBook,
  opts: {
    readonly claudePath?: string | null;
    readonly project?: Partial<ProjectInput>;
    readonly terminals?: (engine: TestEngine) => TerminalHost;
    /** The fake claude prints nothing until it is spoken to. */
    readonly silentClaude?: boolean;
  } = {},
): Promise<TakeoverHarness> {
  const engine = await createTestEngine(book, opts.project ?? {});
  const claude = createFakeClaude({ silent: opts.silentClaude ?? false });
  const home = tempDir('takeover-home');
  const git = createGit();
  const terminals =
    opts.terminals?.(engine) ?? createTerminalHost({ clock: engine.clock, logger: silentLogger });
  const launcher = createLauncher({
    base: { PATH: '/usr/bin:/bin', HOME: home.path, ...LEAKY_LOGIN },
    clientApp: 'test',
    keychain: engine.keychain,
  });
  const watcher = createWatcher({
    state: engine.state,
    inbox: engine.inbox,
    git,
    logger: silentLogger,
    clock: engine.clock,
    watch: () => ({ close: () => undefined }),
  });
  /** A takeover service over `host`; a restarted daemon builds a new one over a new host. */
  const build = (host: TerminalHost): Takeover =>
    createTakeover({
    state: engine.state,
    runner: engine.runner,
    git,
    terminals: host,
    launcher,
    accounts: engine.accounts,
    startGate: engine.startGate,
    processes: systemProcesses,
    clock: engine.clock,
    logger: silentLogger,
    claudePath: opts.claudePath === undefined ? claude.path : opts.claudePath,
    onWorkQueued: () => undefined,
  });
  const hosts: TerminalHost[] = [terminals];
  const takeover = build(terminals);
  const git$ = (...args: string[]): string =>
    execFileSync('git', ['-C', engine.repoPath, ...args], { stdio: 'pipe' })
      .toString()
      .trim();

  function watch(id: WorkstreamId): Pane {
    let text = '';
    const ends: TerminalEnd[] = [];
    const control = takeover.attach(id, {
      output: (data) => {
        text += data;
      },
      exited: () => undefined,
      close: () => undefined,
    });
    // The host reports the raw end too, which the tests read to see a SIGKILL.
    const raw = terminals.attach(id, {
      output: () => undefined,
      ended: (end) => {
        ends.push(end);
      },
      close: () => undefined,
    });
    if (!control || !raw) throw new Error(`no terminal for ${id}`);
    return {
      text: () => text,
      ends,
      input: (data) => {
        control.input(data);
      },
      detach: () => {
        control.detach();
        raw.detach();
      },
    };
  }

  function terminalIs(id: WorkstreamId, status: TerminalStatus): Promise<WorkstreamDto> {
    return waitFor(() => {
      const ws = getWorkstream(engine.store.db, id);
      if (ws.takeover?.terminal !== status) return undefined;
      return status === 'open' && ws.takeover.terminalPid === null ? undefined : ws;
    });
  }

  return {
    engine,
    claude,
    terminals,
    launcher,
    takeover,
    watcher,
    git$,
    commitInTerminal(path) {
      mkdirSync(dirname(join(engine.repoPath, path)), { recursive: true });
      writeFileSync(join(engine.repoPath, path), `${path}\n`);
      git$('add', path);
      git$('commit', '-q', '-m', `Add ${path}`);
      return git$('rev-parse', 'HEAD');
    },
    async running(id) {
      await waitFor(
        () =>
          getWorkstream(engine.store.db, id).state === 'RUNNING' &&
          latestConversation(engine.store.db, id) !== null,
      );
    },
    terminalIs,
    watch,
    async type(id, line) {
      const pane = watch(id);
      pane.input(`${line}\r`);
      await waitFor(() => pane.text().includes(`you said: ${line}`));
      pane.detach();
    },
    async quit(id) {
      const pane = watch(id);
      pane.input('/exit\r');
      const closed = await terminalIs(id, 'closed');
      pane.detach();
      return closed;
    },
    restartTerminals() {
      const host = createTerminalHost({ clock: engine.clock, logger: silentLogger });
      hosts.push(host);
      return build(host);
    },
    async close() {
      const stopping = Promise.all(hosts.map((host) => host.stopAll()));
      engine.clock.advance(60_000);
      await stopping;
      watcher.stop();
      await engine.close();
      claude.cleanup();
      home.cleanup();
    },
  };
}
```

Task 8 adds `reviewWrites` to the `createTakeover` call here.

```ts

import { join } from 'node:path';

const notice = { do: 'await_message' as const, contains: 'taking this session over' };
const turn = { do: 'turn_complete' as const };
const hang = { do: 'hang' as const };
/** A session that stops at the end of its turn once it is told about the takeover. */
const pausable = [notice, turn, hang];
const askShip = {
  do: 'tool' as const,
  toolName: 'AskUserQuestion',

    questions: [
      {
        question: 'Ship it?',
        header: 'Ship',
        options: [
          { label: 'Yes', description: '' },
          { label: 'No', description: '' },
        ],
        multiSelect: false,

};

let h: TakeoverHarness;

  await h.close();
});

const openCard = () => listInbox(h.engine.store.db, { states: ['OPEN'] })[0];

async function launched(title = 'Alpha', areas = ['api']): Promise<WorkstreamId> {
  const { workstream } = h.engine.addTask(title, areas);
  h.engine.launch(workstream.id);
  await h.running(workstream.id);
  return workstream.id;
}

describe('taking over', () => {
  it('pauses a running session and opens its conversation in a terminal the daemon owns', async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [pausable] });
    const id = await launched();

    const taken = await h.takeover.takeOver(id);

    expect(taken).toMatchObject({ state: 'TAKEN_OVER', takeover: { terminal: 'open', lastExit: null } });
    const open = await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');
    const provider = open.takeover?.providerSessionId;
    const pane = h.watch(id);
    await waitFor(() => pane.text().includes('fake claude: ready'));
    expect(pane.text()).toContain('10db: you are driving "Alpha"');
    expect(h.claude.lastRun()).toMatchObject({

        'sonnet',
        '--effort',

        TERMINAL_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
      ],
      cwd: realpathSync(h.engine.repoPath),
    });
    expect(
      listSessions(h.engine.store.db, id).map((s) => [s.mode, s.providerSessionId, s.endedAt === null]),
    ).toEqual([
      ['managed', provider, false],
      ['terminal', provider, true],
    ]);
  });

  it("runs claude in the launcher's environment for the conversation's account, and nothing of the login shell's billing", async () => {

    await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');

    const env = h.claude.lastRun()?.env;
    const project = getProject(h.engine.store.db, ws(id).projectId);
    const expected = h.launcher.session(getAccountRow(h.engine.store.db, project.accountId), 'sonnet').env;
    for (const [name, value] of Object.entries(expected)) expect([name, env?.get(name)]).toEqual([name, value]);
    for (const name of Object.keys(LEAKY_LOGIN)) expect([name, env?.get(name)]).toEqual([name, expected[name]]);
    expect(env?.get('TERM')).toBe('xterm-256color');

    const { workstream } = h.engine.addTask('Alpha');
    h.engine.state.write((ctx) => {
      for (const to of path) transitionWorkstream(ctx, workstream.id, { to, fence: null });
      if (verify) patchWorkstream(ctx, workstream.id, null, { nextAction: { kind: 'verify' } });
    });
    const refused = h.takeover.takeOver(workstream.id);
    await expect(refused).rejects.toThrow(ConflictError);
    await expect(refused).rejects.toThrow(message);
    expect(h.claude.lastRun()).toBeNull();
  });

  it.each([
    {
      name: 'no claude on the login PATH',
      claudePath: () => null,
      error: InvalidError,
      message: 'Claude Code was not found on your login PATH',
    },
    {
      name: 'a claude that cannot be run',
      claudePath: () => {
        const dir = tempDir('not-runnable');
        const path = join(dir.path, 'claude');
        writeFileSync(path, '#!/bin/sh\n');
        chmodSync(path, 0o644);
        return path;
      },
      error: InvalidError,
      message: "can't be run",
    },
    {
      name: 'an account that is paused',
      claudePath: () => undefined,
      pause: true,
      error: ConflictError,
      message: 'is paused',
    },
  ])(
    'refuses a takeover the terminal could not run, and leaves the session running: $name',
    async ({ claudePath, pause, error, message }) => {
      const path = claudePath();
      h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [pausable] }, path === undefined ? {} : { claudePath: path });
      const id = await launched();
      if (pause) {
        const project = getProject(h.engine.store.db, ws(id).projectId);
        h.engine.accounts.refused(getAccountRow(h.engine.store.db, project.accountId), project.id, {
          problem: 'signed_out',
          message: 'signed out',
          seenIdentity: null,
        });
      }
      const refused = h.takeover.takeOver(id);
      await expect(refused).rejects.toThrow(error);
      await expect(refused).rejects.toThrow(message);
      expect(ws(id).state).toBe('RUNNING');
      expect(h.engine.runner.isRunning(id)).toBe(true);
    },
  );

  it("starts a fresh conversation on the project's account, briefed from the step's handoff, when the project left the account its conversation ran on", async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [pausable] });
    const db = h.engine.store.db;
    const client = seedAccount(h.engine.store, { kind: 'claude_subscription', label: 'Client' });
    h.engine.state.write((ctx) => {
      patchAccount(ctx, client.id, { expectedIdentity: 'client@example.com' });
    });
    const id = await launched();
    const earlier = latestConversation(db, id);
    // The project moves while the lane runs; the running session finishes on the account it began on.
    h.engine.state.write((ctx) => {
      patchProject(ctx, ws(id).projectId, { accountId: client.id });
    });

    await h.takeover.takeOver(id);
    await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');

    const run = h.claude.lastRun();
    const fresh = run?.args[1] ?? '';
    expect(fresh).toMatch(/^[0-9a-f]{8}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{4}-[0-9a-f]{12}$/);
    expect(fresh).not.toBe(earlier?.providerSessionId);
    expect(run?.args.slice(0, 8)).toEqual([
      '--session-id',
      fresh,
      '--model',
      'sonnet',
      '--effort',
      'medium',
      '--append-system-prompt',
      TERMINAL_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
    ]);
    // The step has no handoff yet, so the briefing says to look at git first, as a moved lane's does.
    expect(run?.args[8]).toContain('Task: Alpha');
    expect(run?.args[8]).toContain('inspect git status first');
    expect(run?.env.get('CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR')).toBe(getAccountRow(db, client.id).configDir ?? undefined);
    expect(ws(id).takeover).toMatchObject({
      providerSessionId: fresh,
      accountId: client.id,
      freshFrom: null,
    });
    expect(listSessions(db, id).at(-1)).toMatchObject({
      mode: 'terminal',
      providerSessionId: fresh,
      accountId: client.id,
    });
    const earlierLabel = getAccountRow(db, earlier?.accountId ?? client.id).label;
    expect(h.watch(id).text()).toContain(
      `the earlier conversation ran on account "${earlierLabel}", which this project has left`,
    );

    // Hand-back resumes the terminal's own conversation, on the account it ran on.
    await h.quit(id);
    expect((await h.takeover.handBack(id)).nextAction).toMatchObject({
      kind: 'resume',
      providerSessionId: fresh,
    });
  });

  it("holds the subscription account's start gate from spawn until Claude first prints", async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [pausable] });
    const id = await launched();
    const accountId = getProject(h.engine.store.db, ws(id).projectId).accountId;
    const held = await h.engine.startGate.acquire(accountId);

    const taking = h.takeover.takeOver(id);
    await waitFor(() => h.engine.startGate.waiting(accountId) === 1);
    expect(h.claude.lastRun()).toBeNull();
    held();
    await taking;
    await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');

    // Claude printed, so the next start on the account goes ahead.
    (await h.engine.startGate.acquire(accountId))();
  });

  it('lets the start gate go after TERMINAL_START_GATE_MS when Claude prints nothing', async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [pausable] }, { silentClaude: true });
    const id = await launched();
    const accountId = getProject(h.engine.store.db, ws(id).projectId).accountId;
    await h.takeover.takeOver(id);
    await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');

    const next = h.engine.startGate.acquire(accountId);
    expect(h.engine.startGate.waiting(accountId)).toBe(1);
    h.engine.clock.advance(TERMINAL_START_GATE_MS);
    (await next)();
  });

  it('answers 409 when the session ends some other way first', async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [[hang]] });
    const id = await launched();
    const taking = h.takeover.takeOver(id);
    await h.engine.runner.cancel(id);
    await expect(taking).rejects.toThrow('"Alpha" is CANCELLED now');
    expect(h.claude.lastRun()).toBeNull();
  });

  it('refuses a second takeover in the same project, naming the first', async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [[hang]], Beta: [pausable] });
    const alpha = await launched('Alpha');
    const beta = await launched('Beta', ['ui']);

    // Alpha is still on its way to a safe point: the refusal holds before it is taken over, and after.
    const taking = h.takeover.takeOver(alpha);
    await expect(h.takeover.takeOver(beta)).rejects.toThrow(
      '"Alpha" is already taken over in this project',
    );
    // A second click on Alpha itself is told it is on its way, not to hand back what isn't taken yet.
    await expect(h.takeover.takeOver(alpha)).rejects.toThrow('"Alpha" is being taken over;');
    h.engine.clock.advance(DEFAULT_RUNNER_LIMITS.takeoverWaitMs);
    expect((await taking).state).toBe('TAKEN_OVER');
    const refused = h.takeover.takeOver(beta);
    await expect(refused).rejects.toThrow(ConflictError);
    await expect(refused).rejects.toThrow('"Alpha" is already taken over in this project');

    expect(ws(beta).state).toBe('RUNNING');
  });

  it('records why the terminal could not start, and opens it again on request', async () => {
    let failures = 1;
    h = await createTakeoverHarness(
      { Alpha: [pausable] },
      {
        terminals: (engine) =>
          createTerminalHost({
            clock: engine.clock,
            logger: silentLogger,
            // The PTY library is the boundary: this is how it reports a spawn-helper it can't run.
            spawner: (file, args, options) => {
              if (failures > 0) {
                failures -= 1;
                throw new Error('posix_spawnp failed.');
              }
              return nodePtySpawner(file, args, options);
            },
          }),
      },
    );
    const id = await launched();

    const failed = await h.takeover.takeOver(id);

    expect(failed).toMatchObject({
      state: 'TAKEN_OVER',
      currentSessionId: null,
      takeover: {
        terminal: 'closed',
        lastExit: { reason: 'spawn_failed', exitCode: null, signal: null, message: 'posix_spawnp failed.' },
      },
    });
    expect(listSessions(h.engine.store.db, id).at(-1)?.exitReason).toBe('terminal_spawn_failed');
    const again = await h.takeover.takeOver(id);
    expect(again.takeover).toMatchObject({ terminal: 'open', lastExit: null });
    await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');
  });

  it('refuses to open it again while Claude runs in the terminal', async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [pausable] });
    const id = await launched();
    await h.takeover.takeOver(id);
    await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');
    await expect(h.takeover.takeOver(id)).rejects.toThrow(
      /Claude is still running in this workstream's terminal \(process \d+\)\. Quit it with \/exit, then open it again\./,
    );
    expect(listSessions(h.engine.store.db, id).filter((s) => s.mode === 'terminal')).toHaveLength(1);
  });

  it('marks the terminal closed when Claude exits, and keeps the takeover', async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [pausable] });
    const id = await launched();
    await h.takeover.takeOver(id);
    await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');

    const closed = await h.quit(id);

    // A closed terminal is nobody's current session, so nothing later ends it again.
    expect(closed).toMatchObject({
      state: 'TAKEN_OVER',
      currentSessionId: null,
      takeover: { terminal: 'closed', lastExit: { reason: 'exited', exitCode: 0, signal: null, message: null } },
    });
    expect(listSessions(h.engine.store.db, id).at(-1)?.exitReason).toBe('terminal_exited');
  });

  it('keeps Claude running when its pane detaches, and a new pane sees the recent output', async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [pausable] });
    const id = await launched();
    await h.takeover.takeOver(id);
    await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');

    await h.type(id, 'hello');

    expect(h.terminals.isRunning(id)).toBe(true);
    expect(ws(id).takeover?.terminal).toBe('open');
    expect(h.watch(id).text()).toContain('you said: hello');
  });
});
```
