In the accounts plan's cap-card case in `apps/daemon/src/accounts/runtime.test.ts` (`pauses at the cap until the next local month, with one card and account.cap_reached`), add an assertion that the card's body contains `A takeover terminal's spend isn't counted.`

- [ ] **Step 3: Run the tests to verify they fail**

Run: `pnpm --filter @10db/daemon exec vitest run src/takeover src/briefing src/accounts`
Expected: FAIL. `service.test.ts` fails to load because `../src/takeover` has no `createTakeover`, `TERMINAL_SYSTEM_PROMPT` or `TERMINAL_START_GATE_MS`; `briefing.test.ts` has no `terminalBriefing`; the cap card's body lacks the sentence. Four cases pin rules the service code below states on purpose; check each goes red by leaving its line out once: without the `nextAction` check in `takeable` the gate row is refused with the provider-session message instead; without `refuseAnotherTakeover` Beta is taken over too, and without its same-workstream branch the second click on Alpha is told Alpha "is already taken over in this project"; without the `currentSessionId` patch in `closeTerminal` the closed row keeps its terminal session id; without `accountId` in `open`'s `insertSession` the fresh-conversation case finds the terminal row on the account the session started on. Two more: without the `freshFrom` switch in `open`'s write the terminal runs `--resume` on the old conversation and the fresh-conversation case fails on its first argument; without `releaseStart` on the first output the gate case's last `acquire` never resolves and the case times out.

- [ ] **Step 4: Write the prompt, the arguments and the banner**

`apps/daemon/src/takeover/prompt.ts`:

```ts
import type { Effort } from '@10db/contracts';

/**
 * Appended to the terminal conversation's system prompt. A resumed transcript still lists 10db's MCP
 * tools, which a terminal session doesn't have, so its first commit would reach for them.
 */
export const TERMINAL_SYSTEM_PROMPT =
  "The builder now drives this session from a terminal, outside 10db's control. 10db's MCP tools (commit, write_handoff, ask_human and the rest of mcp__10db__) are unavailable here: commit with plain git, ask the builder directly, and do not write a handoff.";

/**
 * The conversation a terminal opens: the one the workstream ran, or a fresh one with a known id,
 * briefed from the step's handoff, when that one lives on an account the project has left.
 */
export type TerminalConversation =
  | { readonly kind: 'resume'; readonly providerSessionId: string }
  | { readonly kind: 'fresh'; readonly sessionId: string; readonly briefing: string };

/** `claude`'s arguments for a takeover: the conversation, the model and effort, and the note. */
export function terminalArgs(input: {
  readonly conversation: TerminalConversation;
  readonly model: string;
  readonly effort: Effort;
}): string[] {
  const options = [
    '--model',
    input.model,
    '--effort',
    input.effort,
    '--append-system-prompt',
    TERMINAL_SYSTEM_PROMPT,
  ];
  switch (input.conversation.kind) {
    case 'resume':
      return ['--resume', input.conversation.providerSessionId, ...options];
    case 'fresh':
      // The id is chosen here, so hand-back resumes this conversation without asking Claude for it.
      return ['--session-id', input.conversation.sessionId, ...options, input.conversation.briefing];
  }
}

/** What the builder reads in the pane before Claude starts: whose work it is, where, and the checkout's rules. */
export function takeoverBanner(input: {
  readonly taskTitle: string;
  readonly workstreamId: string;
  readonly areas: readonly string[];
  readonly accountLabel: string;
  /** The account the earlier conversation ran on, when this one starts fresh; null otherwise. */
  readonly earlierAccountLabel: string | null;
}): string[] {
  return [
    `10db: you are driving "${input.taskTitle}" (${input.workstreamId}) on account "${input.accountLabel}".`,
    ...(input.earlierAccountLabel === null
      ? []
      : [
          `10db: the earlier conversation ran on account "${input.earlierAccountLabel}", which this project has left; this one starts from its handoff.`,
        ]),
    `10db: its claims are ${input.areas.length > 0 ? input.areas.join(', ') : 'none'}; keep your edits inside them.`,
    "10db: 10db's guard is off here. Commit with git as usual; the commits are credited to this task when you hand back.",
    "10db: other sessions may share this checkout. Don't switch branches, stash or reset.",
    '10db: closing this pane leaves Claude running. Quit Claude with /exit, then press Hand back.',
  ];
}
```

In `apps/daemon/src/briefing/prompts.ts`, the `continue_from_handoff` arm of `actionSection` moves into a function both prompts use, and `terminalBriefing` is added (export it from `briefing/index.ts`):

```ts
/** A fresh session's continuation: the step's latest handoff, or git status when there is none. */
function continuation(handoff: HandoffDto | null): string {
  return handoff
    ? `Continue from the handoff below. Do not redo finished work.\n\nHandoff:\n${renderHandoff(handoff)}`
    : 'Continue the task; the previous session left no handoff, so inspect git status first.';
}

/**
 * The first prompt of a fresh terminal conversation, started because the project left the account
 * its conversation ran on: the task and the continuation a moved lane gets (accounts spec §6). No
 * rules about 10db's tools: the terminal has none, and its system prompt says so.
 */
export function terminalBriefing(input: {
  readonly task: Pick<TaskDto, 'title' | 'description' | 'acceptanceCriteria'>;
  readonly claims: readonly { readonly kind: ClaimKind; readonly name: string }[];
  readonly handoff: HandoffDto | null;
}): string {
  const claims = input.claims.map((c) => `${c.kind} ${c.name}`).join(', ') || '(none)';
  return [
    'You are continuing one task in a shared repository. The builder drives this conversation from a terminal; it starts fresh because the earlier one ran on an account this project has left.',
    '',
    `Task: ${input.task.title}`,
    input.task.description,
    '',
    'Acceptance criteria:',
    bullets(input.task.acceptanceCriteria),
    '',
    `Your claims: ${claims}.`,
    '',
    continuation(input.handoff),
  ].join('\n');
}
```

`actionSection`'s `continue_from_handoff` case becomes `return continuation(input.handoff);`. If the accounts plan's `fromHandoff` helper is in the file, `continuation` uses it for the handoff text; the words stay the same.

In `apps/daemon/src/accounts/cards.ts`'s `capCard`, end the body with ` A takeover terminal's spend isn't counted.` (10db doesn't see a terminal's usage; the open item says how a later task could.)

- [ ] **Step 5: Write the service**

`apps/daemon/src/takeover/service.ts`:

```ts
import { randomUUID } from 'node:crypto';
import { accessSync, constants } from 'node:fs';
import {
  AccountKind,
  ConflictError,
  EventType,
  InboxKind,
  InvalidError,
  SessionMode,
  TAKEABLE_STATES,
  TerminalEndReason,
  TerminalStatus,
  WorkstreamState as S,
  type AccountId,
  type ProjectId,
  type SessionId,
  type TakeoverRecord,
  type TerminalExit,
  type TerminalSize,
  type WorkstreamDto,
  type WorkstreamId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  activeClaims,
  endSession,
  enterTakeover,
  getAccountRow,
  getProject,
  getSession,
  getSessionByProviderId,
  getTask,
  getWorkstream,
  getWorkstreamRow,
  insertSession,
  latestConversation,
  latestEventSeq,
  latestHandoffForStep,
  listWorkstreams,
  markSessionStarted,
  patchWorkstream,
  setSessionProcess,
  type AccountRow,
  type DbReader,
  type SessionRow,
  type WorkstreamRow,
  type WriteContext,
} from '@10db/db';
import { unready, type AccountRuntime, type StartGate } from '../accounts';
import { terminalBriefing } from '../briefing';
import type { Clock } from '../clock';
import { AccountLaunchError, type Launcher, type SessionLaunch } from '../environment';
import type { StateStore } from '../events';
import type { Git } from '../git';
import { cancelWorkstreamCards } from '../inbox';
import type { Logger } from '../log';
import { mintSessionToken } from '../mcp';
import type { ProcessTerminator } from '../recovery';
import type { Runner } from '../runner';
import { TerminalSpawnError, type TerminalEnd, type TerminalHost } from '../terminal';
import { takeoverBanner, terminalArgs, type TerminalConversation } from './prompt';

/** The longest a terminal holds its subscription account's start gate when Claude prints nothing. */
export const TERMINAL_START_GATE_MS = 30_000;

/** The cards a session waits on; the terminal session asks again in its own way. */
const WAIT_KINDS: readonly InboxKind[] = [InboxKind.Question, InboxKind.Permission];

/** Why a terminal session's row ended, by how the terminal ended. */
const SESSION_END: Readonly<Record<TerminalEndReason, string>> = {
  exited: 'terminal_exited',
  spawn_failed: 'terminal_spawn_failed',
  daemon_shutdown: 'daemon_shutdown',
  daemon_restart: 'daemon_restart',
};

/** A pane's socket, as the API layer drives it: output and the end in, keystrokes and sizes out. */
export interface TerminalSink {
  output(data: string): void;
  exited(exit: TerminalExit): void;
  close(): void;
}
export interface TerminalControl {
  input(data: string): void;
  resize(size: TerminalSize): void;
  detach(): void;
}

export interface TakeoverDeps {
  readonly state: StateStore;
  readonly runner: Pick<Runner, 'isRunning' | 'takeOver'>;
  readonly git: Git;
  readonly terminals: Pick<TerminalHost, 'open' | 'attach' | 'isRunning' | 'forget'>;
  /** Builds the terminal's environment: the accounts plan's one owner of account variables. */
  readonly launcher: Pick<Launcher, 'session'>;
  readonly accounts: Pick<AccountRuntime, 'blocked'>;
  /** One start at a time on a subscription account (accounts spec §10), shared with managed sessions. */
  readonly startGate: Pick<StartGate, 'acquire'>;
  readonly processes: Pick<ProcessTerminator, 'identify'>;
  readonly clock: Clock;
  readonly logger: Logger;
  /** The installed `claude`; null when none was found, which refuses every takeover. */
  readonly claudePath: string | null;
  /** Work was queued or claims were freed: the scheduler should look again. */
  onWorkQueued(): void;
}

export interface Takeover {
  /**
   * Pauses the managed session at a safe point and opens its conversation in a terminal the daemon
   * owns (spec §5.4). On a taken-over workstream whose terminal closed, opens it again.
   */
  takeOver(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<WorkstreamDto>;
  /** Connects a pane to the workstream's terminal; null when 10db has no terminal for it this run. */
  attach(id: WorkstreamId, sink: TerminalSink): TerminalControl | null;
}

/** What a terminal starts with, on the project's current account, checked before the session is touched. */
interface TerminalStart {
  readonly claude: string;
  readonly launch: SessionLaunch;
  readonly account: AccountRow;
}

/** How the terminal ended; at shutdown the signal is the daemon's own, so it isn't recorded. */
function exitOf(end: TerminalEnd): TerminalExit {
  return end.stopped
    ? { reason: TerminalEndReason.DaemonShutdown, exitCode: null, signal: null, message: null }
    : { reason: TerminalEndReason.Exited, exitCode: end.exitCode, signal: end.signal, message: null };
}

export function createTakeover(deps: TakeoverDeps): Takeover {
  const db = deps.state.db;
  /** The takeover on its way to a safe point, by project: one takeover per project. */
  const takingOver = new Map<ProjectId, { readonly id: WorkstreamId; readonly title: string }>();

  function claudePath(): string {
    if (deps.claudePath === null)
      throw new InvalidError(
        'Claude Code was not found on your login PATH, so there is nothing to open the session with.',
      );
    try {
      accessSync(deps.claudePath, constants.X_OK);
    } catch {
      throw new InvalidError(
        `${deps.claudePath} can't be run; reinstall Claude Code or point TENDB_CLAUDE_PATH at it.`,
      );
    }
    return deps.claudePath;
  }

  /**
   * The terminal runs on the project's current account, which must be able to start a session now,
   * as a managed one must; the launcher reads its secret and refuses one that is missing or changed.
   */
  function terminalStart(row: WorkstreamRow): TerminalStart {
    const claude = claudePath();
    const project = getProject(db, row.projectId);
    const account = getAccountRow(db, project.accountId);
    const named = `Account "${account.label}"`;
    if (account.archived)
      throw new ConflictError(`${named} is archived, so the conversation it holds can't be opened.`);
    const notReady = unready(account);
    if (notReady) throw new ConflictError(`${named} isn't ready: ${notReady.message}`);
    const pause = deps.accounts.blocked(account, project.id);
    if (pause)
      throw new ConflictError(
        `${named} is paused (${pause.reason}${pause.until ? ` until ${pause.until.toISOString()}` : ''}); take the work over once it resumes.`,
      );
    let launch: SessionLaunch;
    try {
      launch = deps.launcher.session(account, row.model);
    } catch (error) {
      if (error instanceof AccountLaunchError) throw new ConflictError(error.message);
      throw error;
    }
    return { claude, launch, account };
  }

  /** The workstream's terminal session while it runs; `reader` is `ctx.tx` inside a write. */
  function liveTerminal(reader: DbReader, row: WorkstreamRow): SessionRow | null {
    if (row.currentSessionId === null) return null;
    const session = getSession(reader, row.currentSessionId);
    return session.mode === SessionMode.Terminal && session.endedAt === null ? session : null;
  }

  /** The refusal for a second driver while Claude runs in the terminal. */
  function stillRunning(session: SessionRow, then: string): ConflictError {
    const which = session.pid === null ? '' : ` (process ${String(session.pid)})`;
    return new ConflictError(
      `Claude is still running in this workstream's terminal${which}. Quit it with /exit, then ${then}.`,
    );
  }

  /** The session has one driver: refuses while Claude runs in the terminal. The write checks again. */
  function refuseWhileRunning(id: WorkstreamId, then: string): void {
    const session = liveTerminal(db, getWorkstreamRow(db, id));
    if (session) throw stillRunning(session, then);
  }

  /**
   * Re-reads the workstream inside a write that acts on a terminal check. Another request may have
   * opened a terminal, handed back or cancelled while this one awaited, so the write goes ahead only
   * while the workstream is still taken over with no terminal running.
   */
  function stillTakenOver(
    ctx: WriteContext,
    id: WorkstreamId,
    then: string,
  ): { readonly row: WorkstreamRow; readonly record: TakeoverRecord } {
    const row = getWorkstreamRow(ctx.tx, id);
    if (row.state !== S.TakenOver || !row.takeover)
      throw new ConflictError(`The workstream is ${row.state} now, no longer taken over.`);
    const live = liveTerminal(ctx.tx, row);
    if (live) throw stillRunning(live, then);
    return { row, record: row.takeover };
  }

  function changed(ctx: WriteContext, id: WorkstreamId, terminal: TerminalStatus): void {
    ctx.emit({
      type: EventType.TakeoverChanged,
      entityType: 'workstream',
      entityId: id,
      data: { terminal },
    });
  }

  /** Ends a terminal session and records how; a second close of the same session does nothing. */
  function closeTerminal(id: WorkstreamId, sessionId: SessionId, exit: TerminalExit): void {
    deps.state.write((ctx) => {
      if (getSession(ctx.tx, sessionId).endedAt !== null) return;
      endSession(ctx, sessionId, SESSION_END[exit.reason]);
      const row = getWorkstreamRow(ctx.tx, id);
      patchWorkstream(ctx, id, null, {
        // A closed terminal is nobody's current session: hand-back and cancel then find none to end.
        ...(row.currentSessionId === sessionId ? { currentSessionId: null } : {}),
        ...(row.takeover ? { takeover: { ...row.takeover, lastExit: exit } } : {}),
      });
      changed(ctx, id, TerminalStatus.Closed);
    });
  }

  /** Records the terminal's process with its start time, so recovery can tell it from a reused pid. */
  async function recordProcess(sessionId: SessionId, pid: number): Promise<void> {
    const startedAt = await deps.processes.identify(pid);
    if (startedAt === null) return;
    deps.state.write((ctx) => {
      if (getSession(ctx.tx, sessionId).endedAt === null)
        setSessionProcess(ctx, sessionId, pid, startedAt);
    });
  }

  /** One takeover per project: two terminals in one checkout would edit the same files. */
  function refuseAnotherTakeover(row: WorkstreamRow): void {
    const pending = takingOver.get(row.projectId);
    // A second click on the same workstream while its takeover is still on its way to a safe point.
    if (pending?.id === row.id)
      throw new ConflictError(
        `"${pending.title}" is being taken over; its terminal opens once the session reaches a safe point.`,
      );
    const other =
      pending?.title ??
      listWorkstreams(db, { states: [S.TakenOver], projectId: row.projectId }).find(
        (w) => w.id !== row.id,
      )?.taskTitle;
    if (other !== undefined)
      throw new ConflictError(
        `"${other}" is already taken over in this project; hand it back or cancel it first. One terminal at a time drives a project's checkout.`,
      );
  }

  /** The conversation a takeover would resume; refuses work that can't be taken over now. */
  function takeable(
    row: WorkstreamRow,
    title: string,
  ): { readonly providerSessionId: string; readonly accountId: AccountId } {
    if (!TAKEABLE_STATES.includes(row.state))
      throw new ConflictError(
        `Only a running, waiting or failed workstream can be opened in a terminal; "${title}" is ${row.state}.`,
      );
    // The gate runs no session, and waiting it out could hold the request for half an hour.
    if (row.state !== S.Failed && row.nextAction.kind === 'verify')
      throw new ConflictError(`The gate is running for "${title}"; take it over after it finishes.`);
    refuseAnotherTakeover(row);
    const conversation = latestConversation(db, row.id);
    if (conversation === null)
      throw new ConflictError(`"${title}" has not started a Claude session yet; try again in a moment.`);
    return conversation;
  }

  /** The workstream's takeover with `freshFrom` cleared, once the fresh conversation exists. */
  function freshStarted(id: WorkstreamId): void {
    deps.state.write((ctx) => {
      const row = getWorkstreamRow(ctx.tx, id);
      if (row.takeover?.freshFrom)
        patchWorkstream(ctx, id, null, { takeover: { ...row.takeover, freshFrom: null } });
    });
  }

  /**
   * Starts a terminal session for a taken-over workstream and spawns `claude` in its PTY, on the
   * project's current account (`start`). The conversation lives in the config directory of the
   * account it ran on, so when the project has left that account the terminal starts a fresh one,
   * briefed from the step's handoff, as a moved lane does (accounts spec §6).
   */
  async function open(id: WorkstreamId, start: TerminalStart): Promise<WorkstreamDto> {
    // Nobody holds this token, so a terminal session never reaches 10db's MCP tools.
    const { hash } = mintSessionToken();
    const { session, row, record, handoff } = deps.state.write((ctx) => {
      // A hand back, a cancel or another open may have landed while this request awaited.
      const current = stillTakenOver(ctx, id, 'open it again');
      const record: TakeoverRecord =
        current.record.accountId === start.account.id
          ? current.record
          : {
              ...current.record,
              providerSessionId: randomUUID(),
              accountId: start.account.id,
              freshFrom: current.record.freshFrom ?? current.record.accountId,
            };
      const latest = latestHandoffForStep(ctx.tx, current.row);
      // A resumed conversation keeps whether it began from a briefing; a fresh one begins from the handoff.
      const continued =
        record.freshFrom === null
          ? getSessionByProviderId(ctx.tx, id, record.providerSessionId)
          : undefined;
      const s = insertSession(ctx, {
        workstreamId: id,
        provider: getProject(ctx.tx, current.row.projectId).provider,
        model: current.row.model,
        effort: current.row.effort,
        mode: SessionMode.Terminal,
        mcpTokenHash: hash,
        accountId: record.accountId,
        briefed: record.freshFrom === null ? (continued?.briefed ?? false) : latest !== null,
        briefingBaselinePct: continued?.briefingBaselinePct ?? null,
      });
      markSessionStarted(ctx, s.id, record.providerSessionId, null);
      patchWorkstream(ctx, id, null, {
        currentSessionId: s.id,
        takeover: { ...record, lastExit: null },
      });
      changed(ctx, id, TerminalStatus.Open);
      return { session: s, row: current.row, record, handoff: latest };
    });
    const task = getTask(db, row.taskId);
    const claims = activeClaims(db, id);
    const conversation: TerminalConversation =
      record.freshFrom === null
        ? { kind: 'resume', providerSessionId: record.providerSessionId }
        : {
            kind: 'fresh',
            sessionId: record.providerSessionId,
            briefing: terminalBriefing({ task, claims, handoff }),
          };
    // Accounts spec §10: a subscription's sessions start one at a time. A terminal never reports
    // `started`, so its first output stands for it, with a deadline for one that prints nothing.
    const release =
      start.account.kind === AccountKind.ClaudeSubscription
        ? await deps.startGate.acquire(start.account.id)
        : () => undefined;
    let released = false;
    const deadline = deps.clock.setTimeout(() => {
      releaseStart();
    }, TERMINAL_START_GATE_MS);
    function releaseStart(): void {
      if (released) return;
      released = true;
      deadline.cancel();
      release();
    }
    try {
      const { pid } = deps.terminals.open(
        id,
        {
          file: start.claude,
          args: terminalArgs({ conversation, model: start.launch.model, effort: row.effort }),
          cwd: getProject(db, row.projectId).repoPath,
          env: { ...start.launch.env, TERM: 'xterm-256color', COLORTERM: 'truecolor' },
          banner: takeoverBanner({
            taskTitle: task.title,
            workstreamId: id,
            areas: claims.map((c) => c.name),
            accountLabel: start.account.label,
            earlierAccountLabel:
              record.freshFrom === null ? null : getAccountRow(db, record.freshFrom).label,
          }),
          onFirstOutput: releaseStart,
        },
        (end) => {
          releaseStart();
          closeTerminal(id, session.id, exitOf(end));
        },
      );
      // Claude creates the fresh conversation from its first prompt; later opens resume it.
      if (record.freshFrom !== null) freshStarted(id);
      void recordProcess(session.id, pid).catch((error: unknown) => {
        deps.logger.warn(
          { workstreamId: id, err: String(error) },
          'reading the terminal start time failed',
        );
      });
    } catch (error) {
      releaseStart();
      if (!(error instanceof TerminalSpawnError)) throw error;
      closeTerminal(id, session.id, {
        reason: TerminalEndReason.SpawnFailed,
        exitCode: null,
        signal: null,
        message: error.message,
      });
    }
    return getWorkstream(db, id);
  }

  /** A workstream with no runner (parked on a card) goes straight to TAKEN_OVER. */
  async function takeOverParked(
    row: WorkstreamRow,
    title: string,
    conversation: { readonly providerSessionId: string; readonly accountId: AccountId },
  ): Promise<void> {
    const repo = getProject(db, row.projectId).repoPath;
    const head = await deps.git.head(repo);
    const branch = await deps.git.currentBranch(repo);
    deps.state.write((ctx) => {
      const now = getWorkstreamRow(ctx.tx, row.id);
      if (now.state !== S.WaitingHuman || deps.runner.isRunning(row.id))
        throw new ConflictError(`"${title}" moved on to ${now.state}; try again.`);
      // In the same write as the transition, so no answer lands on a card the terminal asks again.
      cancelWorkstreamCards(ctx, row.id, WAIT_KINDS);
      enterTakeover(ctx, row.id, null, {
        ...conversation,
        head,
        branch,
        takenAt: ctx.now,
        eventSeq: latestEventSeq(ctx.tx),
        rescued: false,
        freshFrom: null,
        lastExit: null,
      });
    });
  }

  async function takeOver(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<WorkstreamDto> {
    const row = getWorkstreamRow(db, id);
    const title = getTask(db, row.taskId).title;
    if (row.state === S.TakenOver && row.takeover) {
      refuseWhileRunning(id, 'open it again');
      return open(id, terminalStart(row));
    }
    // Everything that can refuse runs before the managed session is touched, with no await between the
    // check and the runner, so the state checked is the state the runner is asked to stop.
    const conversation = takeable(row, title);
    // Refuses an account that can't start a session before the managed session is touched.
    terminalStart(row);
    // Held until this takeover is settled, so a second one in the project is refused meanwhile.
    takingOver.set(row.projectId, { id, title });
    try {
      if (deps.runner.isRunning(id)) await deps.runner.takeOver(id);
      else await takeOverParked(row, title, conversation);
      const after = getWorkstreamRow(db, id);
      if (after.state !== S.TakenOver)
        throw new ConflictError(`The session was not taken over; "${title}" is ${after.state} now.`);
      // Read again: the project may have moved, or its account paused, during the safe-point wait.
      // A refusal here leaves the takeover standing, with Open terminal again to retry.
      return await open(id, terminalStart(after));
    } finally {
      takingOver.delete(row.projectId);
    }
  }

  return {
    takeOver,
    attach(id, sink) {
      const attachment = deps.terminals.attach(id, {
        output: (data) => {
          sink.output(data);
        },
        ended: (end) => {
          sink.exited(exitOf(end));
        },
        close: () => {
          sink.close();
        },
      });
      return attachment;
    },
  };
}
```

  - The watcher watches projects with `TAKEN_OVER` work. A taken-over workstream counts as running for foreign-claim writes whether or not Claude runs in its terminal: after `/exit` and before hand-back, the builder may still edit inside his claims by hand. Every unfinished workstream's claims excuse a write at another lane's step end, a taken-over one's included.

In `apps/daemon/src/keep-awake/index.ts`, import `TerminalStatus` and `WorkstreamState` from `@10db/contracts` and `listWorkstreams` from `@10db/db`, and change `wanted`:

```ts
/** Claude running in a taken-over terminal can work for a long time unattended, like a lane. */
const terminalOpen = (): boolean =>
  listWorkstreams(deps.state.db, { states: [WorkstreamState.TakenOver] }).some(
    (w) => w.takeover?.terminal === TerminalStatus.Open,
  );

const wanted = (): boolean => {
  const current = mode();
  if (current === KeepAwakeMode.Always) return true;
  return (
    current === KeepAwakeMode.WhileRunning &&
    (countLanes(deps.state.db).global > 0 || terminalOpen())
  );
};
```

Task 11 refreshes keep-awake on `takeover.changed` as well as on `workstream.*`.

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 2's `commitShasRecordedAfter`; Task 3's `Git.isAncestor`, `Git.commitFiles`; Task 5's `cancelWorkstreamCards`; Task 6's service internals (`refuseWhileRunning`, `stillTakenOver`, and `closeTerminal` clearing `currentSessionId`); Task 4's `TerminalHost.forget`; Task 7's `Watcher.stepEnd(id, committed)`.
- Produces:
  - `takeover/reconcile.ts`:
    - `HandBackFacts` `{ record: TakeoverRecord; head: string; branch: string | null; kept: boolean; batchBranch: string | null; repoPath: string }`
    - `handBackRefusal(facts: HandBackFacts): string | null`
    - `creditedCommits(all: readonly string[], recordedByOthers: ReadonlySet<string>): string[]`
    - `handBackReason(commits: number): string`
  - `Takeover.handBack(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<WorkstreamDto>` and `Takeover.cancel(id: WorkstreamId): WorkstreamDto` (it reads no git, so it awaits nothing). Hand back checks the terminal before it reads git, then re-reads the workstream inside its write (`stillTakenOver`) and refuses if a terminal opened meanwhile. Neither ends a session: a closed terminal already ended its own and is no longer current. Both drop the terminal's scrollback (`terminals.forget`), which closes any pane still attached.
  - `TakeoverDeps.reviewWrites?: (id: WorkstreamId, committed: readonly string[]) => Promise<void>` (the watcher's step-end check; absent when the watcher is off).
  - `MAX_LISTED_FILES = 200` (dirty files in the `handed_back` recovery step).
  - Task 9 fills `batchBranch`; until then the service passes `null`.

```ts
import { mintId, type TakeoverRecord } from '@10db/contracts';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { creditedCommits, handBackReason, handBackRefusal, type HandBackFacts } from './reconcile';

const record: TakeoverRecord = {
  providerSessionId: 'provider-1',
  accountId: mintId('Account'),
  head: 'a'.repeat(40),
  branch: 'main',
  takenAt: '2026-10-22T12:00:00.000Z',
  eventSeq: 0,
  rescued: false,
  freshFrom: null,
  lastExit: null,
};
const facts = (overrides: Partial<HandBackFacts> = {}): HandBackFacts => ({
  record,
  head: 'b'.repeat(40),
  branch: 'main',
  kept: true,
  batchBranch: null,
  repoPath: '/repo',
  ...overrides,
});

describe('handBackRefusal', () => {
  it.each([
    { name: 'the takeover branch, with the takeover point kept', facts: facts(), refusal: null },
    {
      name: 'another branch',
      facts: facts({ branch: 'side' }),
      refusal:
        'The checkout is on side, but 10db took the session over on main. Run git switch main in /repo, then hand back.',
    },
    {
      name: 'a detached HEAD',
      facts: facts({ branch: null }),
      refusal:
        'The checkout is detached at bbbbbbbb, but 10db took the session over on main. Run git switch main in /repo, then hand back.',
    },
    {
      name: 'history without the takeover point',
      facts: facts({ kept: false }),
      refusal:
        'aaaaaaaa, where you took the session over, is no longer in the history of main: a reset, rebase or amend dropped commits. Restore them (git reflog shows them) and hand back, or cancel the workstream.',
    },
    {
      name: 'the takeover branch when the batch branch differs',
      facts: facts({ batchBranch: '10db/greetings-0a1b2c3d' }),
      refusal:
        'The checkout is on main, but the batch branch is 10db/greetings-0a1b2c3d. Run git switch 10db/greetings-0a1b2c3d in /repo, then hand back.',
    },
    {
      name: 'the batch branch',
      facts: facts({
        record: { ...record, branch: '10db/g-1' },
        branch: '10db/g-1',
        batchBranch: '10db/g-1',
      }),
      refusal: null,
    },
    {
      name: 'any branch after a takeover from a detached HEAD outside a batch',
      facts: facts({ record: { ...record, branch: null }, branch: 'side' }),
      refusal: null,
    },
  ])('$name', ({ facts: f, refusal }) => {
    expect(handBackRefusal(f)).toBe(refusal);
  });
});

describe('creditedCommits', () => {
  it('keeps the commits nobody else recorded, in order', () => {
    expect(creditedCommits(['a', 'b', 'c'], new Set(['b']))).toEqual(['a', 'c']);
  });
});

describe('handBackReason', () => {
  it.each([
    { commits: 0, text: 'the builder drove this conversation from a terminal and handed it back' },
    {
      commits: 2,
      text: 'the builder drove this conversation from a terminal and handed it back, with 2 commits of their own',
    },
  ])('names $commits commits', ({ commits, text }) => {
    expect(handBackReason(commits)).toContain(text);
  });
});
```

```ts
const write = (path: string) => ({
  do: 'tool' as const,
  toolName: 'Write',
  input: { file_path: path, content: 'x' },
});
const commit = (opId: string, paths: string[]) => ({
  do: 'mcp' as const,
  tool: 'commit',
  args: { opId, paths, message: `Commit ${opId}` },
});
/** Session 1 is managed, 2 is the terminal (the fake adapter never runs it), 3 is managed again. */
const afterHandBack = [write('src/api/b.txt'), commit('op-hand-0001', ['src/api/b.txt']), turn];
const transitions = (id: WorkstreamId) =>
  listEventsAfter(h.engine.store.db, 0, 10_000)
    .filter((e) => e.type === EventType.WorkstreamChanged && e.entityId === id)
    .map((e) => `${String(e.data['from'])}->${String(e.data['to'])}`);
const eventData = (type: EventType, id: WorkstreamId) =>
  listEventsAfter(h.engine.store.db, 0, 10_000)
    .filter((e) => e.type === type && e.entityId === id)
    .map((e) => e.data);

/** Alpha taken over at the end of its first turn, with Claude open in the terminal. */
async function takenOver(): Promise<WorkstreamId> {
  h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [pausable, [], afterHandBack] });
  const id = await launched();
  await h.takeover.takeOver(id);
  await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');
  return id;
}

async function closeTerminal(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<WorkstreamDto> {
  return h.quit(id);
}

describe('handing back', () => {
  it('refuses while Claude runs in the terminal, then resumes the same conversation in managed mode', async () => {
    const id = await takenOver();
    await expect(h.takeover.handBack(id)).rejects.toThrow(
      /Claude is still running in this workstream's terminal \(process \d+\)\. Quit it with \/exit, then hand back\./,
    );
    const sha = h.commitInTerminal('src/api/t.txt');
    const provider = (await closeTerminal(id)).takeover?.providerSessionId;

    const back = await h.takeover.handBack(id);

    expect(back).toMatchObject({
      state: 'QUEUED',
      takeover: null,
      currentSessionId: null,
      nextAction: { kind: 'resume', providerSessionId: provider },
    });
    expect(transitions(id).slice(-2)).toEqual(['TAKEN_OVER->RECOVERING', 'RECOVERING->QUEUED']);
    expect(eventData(EventType.CommitRecorded, id)).toEqual([
      { sha, paths: ['src/api/t.txt'], alreadyCommitted: false, via: 'terminal' },
    ]);
    // The terminal session keeps the reason it closed with, and ended once.
    const terminalSession = listSessions(h.engine.store.db, id).at(-1);
    expect(terminalSession?.exitReason).toBe('terminal_exited');
    expect(
      listEventsAfter(h.engine.store.db, 0, 10_000).filter(
        (e) => e.type === EventType.SessionEnded && e.entityId === terminalSession?.id,
      ),
    ).toHaveLength(1);
    // The scrollback went with the terminal: no pane can attach any more.
    expect(h.takeover.attach(id, { output: () => undefined, exited: () => undefined, close: () => undefined })).toBeNull();

    h.engine.launch(id);
    await h.engine.runner.idle();
    expect(ws(id).state).toBe('DONE');
    const resumed = h.engine.adapter.prompts.at(-1);
    expect(resumed?.resume).toBe(provider);
    expect(resumed?.prompt).toContain('from a terminal');
  });

  it.each([
    {
      name: 'switched to another branch',
      move: (t: TakeoverHarness) => t.git$('switch', '-q', '-c', 'side'),
      refusal: 'The checkout is on side, but 10db took the session over on main.',
      fix: (t: TakeoverHarness) => t.git$('switch', '-q', 'main'),
    },
    {
      name: 'detached the HEAD',
      move: (t: TakeoverHarness) => t.git$('checkout', '-q', '--detach'),
      refusal: 'The checkout is detached at',
      fix: (t: TakeoverHarness) => t.git$('switch', '-q', 'main'),
    },
    {
      name: 'reset below the takeover point',
      move: (t: TakeoverHarness) => t.git$('reset', '-q', '--hard', 'HEAD~1'),
      refusal: 'where you took the session over, is no longer in the history of main',
      fix: (t: TakeoverHarness) => t.git$('reset', '-q', '--hard', 'ORIG_HEAD'),
    },
  ])(
    'refuses to hand back after the terminal $name, then hands back once it is fixed',
    async ({ move, refusal, fix }) => {
      h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [pausable] });
      // A second commit, so the takeover point has a parent to reset to.
      h.commitInTerminal('src/api/before.txt');
      const id = await launched();
      await h.takeover.takeOver(id);
      await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');
      await closeTerminal(id);
      move(h);
      await expect(h.takeover.handBack(id)).rejects.toThrow(refusal);
      expect(ws(id).state).toBe('TAKEN_OVER');
      fix(h);
      expect((await h.takeover.handBack(id)).state).toBe('QUEUED');
    },
  );

  it('credits only the commits no other workstream recorded', async () => {
    const id = await takenOver();
    const beta = h.engine.addTask('Beta', ['ui']).workstream.id;
    const theirs = h.commitInTerminal('src/ui/theirs.txt');
    h.engine.state.write((ctx) => {
      ctx.emit({
        type: EventType.CommitRecorded,
        entityType: 'workstream',
        entityId: beta,
        data: { sha: theirs, paths: ['src/ui/theirs.txt'], alreadyCommitted: false },
      });
    });
    const mine = h.commitInTerminal('src/api/mine.txt');
    await closeTerminal(id);

    await h.takeover.handBack(id);

    expect(eventData(EventType.CommitRecorded, id).map((d) => d['sha'])).toEqual([mine]);
    expect(eventData(EventType.RecoveryStep, id).at(-1)).toMatchObject({
      step: 'handed_back',
      commits: [mine],
    });
  });

  it('flags a terminal commit outside the claims, and hands back anyway', async () => {
    const id = await takenOver();
    h.commitInTerminal('docs/notes.md');
    await closeTerminal(id);

    expect((await h.takeover.handBack(id)).state).toBe('QUEUED');

    const strays = listInbox(h.engine.store.db, { states: ['OPEN'] }).filter(
      (i) => i.kind === 'flag' && i.title === 'Write outside every claim',
    );
    expect(strays.map((f) => f.body)).toEqual(['docs/notes.md']);
  });

  it('refuses a workstream nobody took over', async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Alpha: [pausable] });
    const id = await launched();
    await expect(h.takeover.handBack(id)).rejects.toThrow(
      'Only a taken-over workstream can be handed back.',
    );
  });
});

describe('cancelling a taken-over workstream', () => {
  it('is refused while Claude runs in the terminal, then cancels and frees the claims', async () => {
    const id = await takenOver();
    expect(() => h.takeover.cancel(id)).toThrow('Quit it with /exit, then cancel');
    expect(activeClaims(h.engine.store.db, id)).toHaveLength(1);
    await closeTerminal(id);

    const cancelled = h.takeover.cancel(id);

    expect(cancelled).toMatchObject({ state: 'CANCELLED', takeover: null, currentSessionId: null });
    expect(activeClaims(h.engine.store.db, id)).toEqual([]);
    expect(h.takeover.attach(id, { output: () => undefined, exited: () => undefined, close: () => undefined })).toBeNull();
  });
});

describe('two requests at once', () => {
  it('refuses to hand back once the terminal was opened again meanwhile, and keeps that terminal', async () => {
    const id = await takenOver();
    await closeTerminal(id);

    // Hand back passes its terminal check and awaits git; the reopen runs to its write in the same tick,
    // so hand back's write is the one that finds a terminal running.
    const acting = h.takeover.handBack(id);
    const reopened = h.takeover.takeOver(id);

    await expect(acting).rejects.toThrow("Claude is still running in this workstream's terminal");
    expect(await reopened).toMatchObject({ state: 'TAKEN_OVER', takeover: { terminal: 'open' } });
    expect(activeClaims(h.engine.store.db, id)).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(transitions(id)).not.toContain('TAKEN_OVER->RECOVERING');
    await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');
  });
});
```

`apps/daemon/src/takeover/reconcile.ts`:

```ts
import type { TakeoverRecord } from '@10db/contracts';

/** What hand-back reads from git and the batch before it gives the conversation back (spec §5.4). */
export interface HandBackFacts {
  readonly record: TakeoverRecord;
  readonly head: string;
  /** The checked-out branch now; null on a detached HEAD. */
  readonly branch: string | null;
  /** Whether the takeover HEAD is still in the history of HEAD. */
  readonly kept: boolean;
  /** The branch of the workstream's unfinished batch; null outside a batch. */
  readonly batchBranch: string | null;
  readonly repoPath: string;
}

const short = (sha: string): string => sha.slice(0, 8);

/** Why the checkout can't be handed back as it is, with the fix; null when it can. */
export function handBackRefusal(f: HandBackFacts): string | null {
  const expected = f.batchBranch ?? f.record.branch;
  const which = f.batchBranch === null ? '10db took the session over on' : 'the batch branch is';
  const fix = (branch: string) => `Run git switch ${branch} in ${f.repoPath}, then hand back.`;
  if (expected !== null && f.branch === null)
    return `The checkout is detached at ${short(f.head)}, but ${which} ${expected}. ${fix(expected)}`;
  if (expected !== null && f.branch !== expected)
    return `The checkout is on ${f.branch ?? 'a detached HEAD'}, but ${which} ${expected}. ${fix(expected)}`;
  if (!f.kept)
    return `${short(f.record.head)}, where you took the session over, is no longer in the history of ${f.branch ?? 'HEAD'}: a reset, rebase or amend dropped commits. Restore them (git reflog shows them) and hand back, or cancel the workstream.`;
  return null;
}

/** The commits since the takeover that other workstreams didn't record through the commit tool. */
export function creditedCommits(
  all: readonly string[],
  recordedByOthers: ReadonlySet<string>,
): string[] {
  return all.filter((sha) => !recordedByOthers.has(sha));
}

/** Why managed mode resumes; the resume prompt quotes it to the agent. */
export function handBackReason(commits: number): string {
  const theirs =
    commits === 0 ? '' : `, with ${String(commits)} commit${commits === 1 ? '' : 's'} of their own`;
  return `the builder drove this conversation from a terminal and handed it back${theirs}; read git status and git log before you continue, and leave their changes in place`;
}
```

```ts
   */
```

- inside `createTakeover`, add:

```ts
async function handBack(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<WorkstreamDto> {
  const row = getWorkstreamRow(db, id);
  const record = row.takeover;
  if (row.state !== S.TakenOver || !record)
    throw new ConflictError('Only a taken-over workstream can be handed back.');
  refuseWhileRunning(id, 'hand back');
  const repo = getProject(db, row.projectId).repoPath;
  const [head, branch, kept] = await Promise.all([
    deps.git.head(repo),
    deps.git.currentBranch(repo),
    deps.git.isAncestor(repo, record.head),
  ]);
  const refusal = handBackRefusal({
    record,
    head,
    branch,
    kept,
    batchBranch: null,
    repoPath: repo,
  });
  if (refusal) throw new ConflictError(refusal);
  const commits = creditedCommits(
    await deps.git.commitsSince(repo, record.head),
    commitShasRecordedAfter(db, record.eventSeq, id),
  );
  const files = await Promise.all(
    commits.map(async (sha) => ({ sha, paths: await deps.git.commitFiles(repo, sha) })),
  );
  const dirty = (await deps.git.status(repo)).map((s) => s.path);
  deps.state.write((ctx) => {
    // A terminal opened again, or a cancel, may have landed while git was read. No session is ended
    // here: a closed terminal ended its own and is no longer the current session.
    stillTakenOver(ctx, id, 'hand back');
    for (const { sha, paths } of files)
      ctx.emit({
        type: EventType.CommitRecorded,
        entityType: 'workstream',
        entityId: id,
        data: { sha, paths, alreadyCommitted: false, via: 'terminal' },
      });
    ctx.emit({
      type: EventType.RecoveryStep,
      entityType: 'workstream',
      entityId: id,
      data: {
        step: 'handed_back',
        head,
        branch,
        commits,
        dirtyFiles: dirty.slice(0, MAX_LISTED_FILES),
      },
    });
    transitionWorkstream(ctx, id, {
      to: S.Recovering,
      fence: null,
      reason: 'handed back from the terminal',
    });
    transitionWorkstream(ctx, id, {
      to: S.Queued,
      fence: null,
      patch: {
        takeover: null,
        currentSessionId: null,
        nextAction: {
          kind: 'resume',
          providerSessionId: record.providerSessionId,
          reason: handBackReason(commits.length),
        },
      },
      reason: 'handed back from the terminal',
    });
  });
  deps.terminals.forget(id);
  if (deps.reviewWrites) {
    try {
      await deps.reviewWrites(
        id,
        files.flatMap((f) => f.paths),
      );
    } catch (error) {
      deps.logger.warn({ workstreamId: id, err: String(error) }, 'hand-back claim check failed');
    }
  }
  deps.onWorkQueued();
  return getWorkstream(db, id);
}

function cancel(id: WorkstreamId): WorkstreamDto {
  if (getWorkstreamRow(db, id).state !== S.TakenOver)
    throw new ConflictError('Only a taken-over workstream is cancelled here.');
  refuseWhileRunning(id, 'cancel');
  deps.state.write((ctx) => {
    stillTakenOver(ctx, id, 'cancel');
    cancelWorkstreamCards(ctx, id);
    transitionWorkstream(ctx, id, {
      to: S.Cancelled,
      fence: null,
      patch: { takeover: null, currentSessionId: null },
      reason: 'cancelled by the builder',
    });
    releaseClaims(ctx, id);
    clearLease(ctx, id);
  });
  deps.terminals.forget(id);
  // Its claims are free: queued work in those areas can launch.
  deps.onWorkQueued();
  return getWorkstream(db, id);
}
```

### Task 9: Shutdowns, restarts and batches keep their rules with a taken-over workstream

**Files:**

- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/recovery/recover.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/takeover/service.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/delivery/start.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/delivery/cards.ts`
- Test: `apps/daemon/src/recovery/recover.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/takeover/service.test.ts` (a new `describe`), `apps/daemon/src/takeover/batch.test.ts` (new)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Tasks 2, 4, 6 and 8; the batch plan's `createDeliveryHarness` (with `starter`, `shipper`, `jobs`, `settle`, `git$`, `repo`), `getBatch`, `transitionBatch`, `UNFINISHED_BATCH_STATES`, `BatchState`, `recoverBatches` and `Shipper.ship`, `abandon`, `checkBatch`; the accounts plan's `createLauncher` and `createMemoryKeychain`.
- Produces:
  - A shutdown (`TerminalHost.stopAll`) closes each running terminal with `lastExit` `daemon_shutdown` and its session ended `daemon_shutdown`; the workstream stays `TAKEN_OVER` (Task 6's `exitOf` already maps a `stopped` end; this task pins it).
  - `recoverOnStartup` keeps a `TAKEN_OVER` workstream in its state. Like any workstream's, its live sessions are ended (`daemon_restart`) and their processes terminated by pid and start time, a terminal's included; a taken-over workstream whose terminal was live also gets `lastExit` `daemon_restart` and a `takeover.changed` event.
  - The takeover service refuses a takeover while the workstream's batch is `CUTTING` or `SHIPPING`, and hand-back checks the batch branch of an unfinished batch (`batchBranch`).
  - A batch cut switches branches only when nobody drives the checkout: it refuses, with `startCards.takenOver(project, title)`, while any workstream in the project is `TAKEN_OVER`, a workstream outside every batch included (say one taken over before the project's delivery became pull requests). Joining an open batch switches nothing, so it doesn't check.

```ts
it('ends a terminal that outlived a crash, and keeps the workstream taken over', async () => {
  // Stands in for a claude whose PTY master died with the old daemon but which ignored the hangup.
  const terminalProcess = spawn('/bin/sleep', ['60'], { stdio: 'ignore' });
  try {
    const pid = terminalProcess.pid ?? -1;
    const pidStart = await waitForAsync(
      async () => (await systemProcesses.identify(pid)) ?? undefined,
    );
    const seeded = seedWorkstream(base, { repoPath: repo.path });
    const terminal = state.write((ctx) => {
      transitionWorkstream(ctx, seeded.workstream.id, { to: 'STARTING', fence: null });
      transitionWorkstream(ctx, seeded.workstream.id, { to: 'RUNNING', fence: null });
      enterTakeover(ctx, seeded.workstream.id, null, {
        providerSessionId: 'provider-1',
        accountId: getProject(ctx.tx, seeded.workstream.projectId).accountId,
        head: 'a'.repeat(40),
        branch: 'main',
        takenAt: ctx.now,
        eventSeq: 0,
        rescued: false,
        freshFrom: null,
        lastExit: null,
      });
      const s = insertSession(ctx, {
        workstreamId: seeded.workstream.id,
        provider: 'claude',
        model: 'sonnet',
        effort: 'medium',
        mode: 'terminal',
        mcpTokenHash: mintId('Session'),
      });
      markSessionStarted(ctx, s.id, 'provider-1', null);
      setSessionProcess(ctx, s.id, pid, pidStart);
      patchWorkstream(ctx, seeded.workstream.id, null, { currentSessionId: s.id });
      return s;
    });

    const report = await recover();

    expect(report).toMatchObject({ requeued: [], failed: [], parked: [] });
    expect(getWorkstream(state.db, seeded.workstream.id)).toMatchObject({
      state: 'TAKEN_OVER',
      currentSessionId: null,
      recoveryAttempts: 0,
      takeover: {
        terminal: 'closed',
        lastExit: { reason: 'daemon_restart', exitCode: null, signal: null, message: null },
      },
    });
    expect(listSessions(state.db, seeded.workstream.id).at(-1)).toMatchObject({
      id: terminal.id,
      exitReason: 'daemon_restart',
    });
    await waitForAsync(async () =>
      (await systemProcesses.identify(pid)) === null ? true : undefined,
    );
  } finally {
    terminalProcess.kill();
  }
});
```

```ts
import { ConflictError, mintId, type BatchDto, type WorkstreamId } from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  endSession,
  getBatch,
  getWorkstream,
  insertSession,
  insertWorkstream,
  listWorkstreams,
  markSessionStarted,
  transitionBatch,
} from '@10db/db';
import { afterEach, beforeEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createDeliveryHarness, type DeliveryHarness } from '../../test/delivery-harness';
import { createFakeClaude, type FakeClaude } from '../../test/fake-claude';
import { createMemoryKeychain, waitFor } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { createStartGate } from '../accounts';
import { systemClock } from '../clock';
import { recoverBatches } from '../delivery';
import { createLauncher } from '../environment';
import { silentLogger } from '../log';
import { recoverOnStartup, systemProcesses } from '../recovery';
import { createTerminalHost, type TerminalHost } from '../terminal';
import { createTakeover, type Takeover } from './index';

let h: DeliveryHarness;
let claude: FakeClaude;
let terminals: TerminalHost;
let takeover: Takeover;
beforeEach(() => {
  h = createDeliveryHarness();
  claude = createFakeClaude();
  terminals = createTerminalHost({ clock: systemClock, logger: silentLogger });
  takeover = createTakeover({
    state: h.state,
    // No runner holds these workstreams: each is parked on a card, or failed, when it is taken over.
    runner: { isRunning: () => false, takeOver: () => Promise.resolve() },
    git: h.git,
    terminals,
    launcher: createLauncher({
      base: { PATH: '/usr/bin:/bin' },
      clientApp: 'test',
      keychain: createMemoryKeychain(),
    }),
    // No account is paused or capped in these tests; the account rules are Task 6's.
    accounts: { blocked: () => null },
    startGate: createStartGate(),
    processes: systemProcesses,
    clock: systemClock,
    logger: silentLogger,
    claudePath: claude.path,
    onWorkQueued: () => undefined,
  });
});
afterEach(async () => {
  await terminals.stopAll();
  claude.cleanup();
  h.close();
});

/** One managed session that began a conversation and parked. */
function conversed(id: WorkstreamId): void {
  h.state.write((ctx) => {
    const s = insertSession(ctx, {
      workstreamId: id,
      provider: 'claude',
      model: 'sonnet',
      effort: 'medium',
      mode: 'managed',
      mcpTokenHash: mintId('Session'),
    });
    markSessionStarted(ctx, s.id, 'provider-1', null);
    endSession(ctx, s.id, 'parked');
  });
}

/** A one-task batch, open on its branch, whose workstream is parked on a card after one session. */
async function parkedInBatch(): Promise<{ batch: BatchDto; id: WorkstreamId }> {
  const task = h.addTask('Alpha');
  const started = h.starter.startBatch(h.project.id, { title: 'Greetings', taskIds: [task.id] });
  await h.jobs.idle();
  const [ws] = listWorkstreams(h.store.db, { batchId: started.id });
  if (!ws) throw new Error('the batch opened no workstream');
  h.settle(ws.id, 'WAITING_HUMAN');
  conversed(ws.id);
  return { batch: getBatch(h.store.db, started.id), id: ws.id };
}

/** Quits Claude in the workstream's terminal, as /exit does. */
async function quit(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<void> {
  const control = takeover.attach(id, {
    output: () => undefined,
    exited: () => undefined,
    close: () => undefined,
  });
  control?.input('/exit\r');
  await waitFor(() => getWorkstream(h.store.db, id).takeover?.terminal === 'closed');
  control?.detach();
}

describe('takeover in a pull-request batch', () => {
  it('cuts no batch while a workstream outside every batch is taken over, and names it', async () => {
    // Taken over before the project's delivery became pull requests: it belongs to no batch.
    const loose = h.addTask('Loose', { areas: ['ui'] });
    const id = h.state.write((c) => insertWorkstream(c, loose.id)).id;
    h.settle(id, 'WAITING_HUMAN');
    conversed(id);
    await takeover.takeOver(id);

    const request = h.starter.startBatch(h.project.id, {
      title: 'Greetings',
      taskIds: [h.addTask('Alpha').id],
    });
    await h.jobs.idle();

    expect(h.cards().map((c) => c.title)).toEqual([
      expect.stringContaining('"Loose" is taken over in a terminal'),
    ]);
    expect(() => getBatch(h.store.db, request.id)).toThrow();
    expect(h.git$('branch', '--show-current')).toBe('main');
  });

  it('refuses to take over while the batch is shipping', async () => {
    const { batch, id } = await parkedInBatch();
    h.state.write((ctx) => transitionBatch(ctx, batch.id, { to: 'SHIPPING' }));
    const refused = takeover.takeOver(id);
    await expect(refused).rejects.toThrow(ConflictError);
    await expect(refused).rejects.toThrow('Batch "Greetings" is shipping');
    expect(claude.lastRun()).toBeNull();
  });

  it('refuses hand back off the batch branch, even when the takeover began off it', async () => {
    const { batch, id } = await parkedInBatch();
    h.git$('switch', '-q', 'main');
    await takeover.takeOver(id);
    await quit(id);
    await expect(takeover.handBack(id)).rejects.toThrow(
      `The checkout is on main, but the batch branch is ${batch.branch ?? ''}.`,
    );
    h.git$('switch', '-q', batch.branch ?? '');
    expect((await takeover.handBack(id)).state).toBe('QUEUED');
  });

  it('holds the batch open, and refuses Ship anyway and Abandon, while a member is taken over', async () => {
    const { batch, id } = await parkedInBatch();
    await takeover.takeOver(id);
    h.shipper.checkBatch(batch.id);
    await h.jobs.idle();
    expect(getBatch(h.store.db, batch.id).state).toBe('OPEN');
    expect(() => h.shipper.ship(batch.id)).toThrow(ConflictError);
    expect(() => h.shipper.abandon(batch.id)).toThrow(/running/);
    expect(getWorkstream(h.store.db, id).state).toBe('TAKEN_OVER');
  });

  it('restarts with the checkout off the batch branch, and comes back through hand back and Retry', async () => {
    const { batch, id } = await parkedInBatch();
    await takeover.takeOver(id);
    await quit(id);
    // The builder moved the checkout in the terminal, then the daemon restarted.
    h.git$('switch', '-q', 'main');
    const recovered = await recoverOnStartup({
      state: h.state,
      inbox: h.inbox,
      git: h.git,
      processes: systemProcesses,
      maxRecoveryAttempts: 3,
      logger: silentLogger,
    });
    await recoverBatches({
      state: h.state,
      git: h.git,
      inbox: h.inbox,
      processes: systemProcesses,
      shipper: h.shipper,
      logger: silentLogger,
    });
    expect(recovered.requeued).toEqual([]);
    expect(getWorkstream(h.store.db, id).state).toBe('TAKEN_OVER');
    expect(getBatch(h.store.db, batch.id)).toMatchObject({
      state: 'FAILED',
      failure: 'wrong_branch',
    });

    await expect(takeover.handBack(id)).rejects.toThrow('the batch branch is');
    h.git$('switch', '-q', batch.branch ?? '');
    expect((await takeover.handBack(id)).state).toBe('QUEUED');
    h.shipper.ship(batch.id);
    await h.jobs.idle();
    expect(getBatch(h.store.db, batch.id).state).toBe('OPEN');
  });
});
```

(`recoverOnStartup`'s and `recoverBatches`'s options are the ones `main` has; where they differ, pass what the code asks for and say so.)

- [ ] **Step 2: Run the tests to verify they fail**

Run: `pnpm --filter @10db/daemon exec vitest run src/recovery src/takeover`
Expected: FAIL.

- The `TAKEN_OVER` table row ends `QUEUED`, and the new recovery case finds the workstream `RECOVERING` or `QUEUED` with no `lastExit`.
- `cuts no batch while a workstream outside every batch is taken over…` opens the batch: the cut counts lanes, and a taken-over workstream holds none.
- `refuses to take over while the batch is shipping` resolves instead of rejecting.
- `refuses hand back off the batch branch…` hands back on `main`.
- `restarts with the checkout off the batch branch…` finds `requeued` holding the workstream.
- `a shutdown` **passes**: it pins Task 6's `exitOf` and the host's `stopping` flag. Prove it can fail: in `exitOf`, return the `Exited` arm for every end, and see the `lastExit` and `exitReason` lines fail; revert. Say so in the task's report.
- `holds the batch open…` **passes**: it pins what the batch plan built. Prove it can fail with two temporary mutations, each reverted at once. - [ ] **Step 3: Keep a taken-over workstream through a restart**

In `apps/daemon/src/recovery/recover.ts` (import `EventType`, `SessionMode`, `TerminalEndReason` and `type TerminalExit` from `@10db/contracts`):

- add `S.TakenOver` to `KEEP_STATE`, and widen its comment: "States that keep their place: only the dead daemon's lease, sessions and processes are cleaned up. A taken-over workstream stays taken over; its terminal died with the old daemon, and the builder opens it again (spec §5.4)."
- add, beside `MAX_DIRTY_FILES`:

```ts
/** How a terminal ends when the daemon that owned its PTY is gone. */
const RESTARTED: TerminalExit = {
  reason: TerminalEndReason.DaemonRestart,
  exitCode: null,
  signal: null,
  message: null,
};
```

- the termination loop stays as it is: it terminates every live session's process by pid and start time, a terminal's too.
- replace the first write with:

```ts
const terminalEnded =
  row.takeover !== null && live.some((s) => s.mode === SessionMode.Terminal);
opts.state.write((ctx) => {
  for (const s of live) endSession(ctx, s.id, 'daemon_restart');
  patchWorkstream(ctx, ws.id, null, {
    currentSessionId: null,
    gatePid: null,
    gatePidStart: null,
    ...(terminalEnded && row.takeover ? { takeover: { ...row.takeover, lastExit: RESTARTED } } : {}),
  });
  if (terminalEnded)
    ctx.emit({
      type: EventType.TakeoverChanged,
      entityType: 'workstream',
      entityId: ws.id,
      data: { terminal: 'closed' },
    });
  clearLease(ctx, ws.id);
});
```

A terminal session found open at startup is always a dead daemon's: its PTY master closed with that daemon, which sent the process SIGHUP. Claude Code exits on it; the termination loop ends one that didn't.

```ts
/** The workstream's batch while it is unfinished; null outside a batch or once it finished. */
function unfinishedBatch(row: WorkstreamRow): BatchDto | null {
  const batch = row.batchId ? getBatch(db, row.batchId) : null;
  return batch && UNFINISHED_BATCH_STATES.includes(batch.state) ? batch : null;
}
```

### Task 10: Rescue a failed workstream in a terminal

A `FAILED` workstream with a started conversation can be taken over when Retry would accept it. Its claims come back in the takeover's write, and hand-back does what Retry does on the same conversation (decision T12).

**Files:**

- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/team/steps.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/team/index.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/takeover/service.ts`
- Test: `apps/daemon/src/takeover/rescue.test.ts` (new), `apps/daemon/src/takeover/batch.test.ts` (a new `describe`)

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Task 1's `FAILED → TAKEN_OVER` edge and `TakeoverRecord.rescued`; Tasks 6, 8 and 9's service (`takeable`, `takeOver`, `handBack`, `unfinishedBatch`); the existing `retryWorkstream`, `restartStep`, `latestHandoffForStep`, `acquireClaims`, `claimRequestsForTask`, `activeClaims`.
- Produces:
  - `team/`: `retryRefusal(db: DbReader, row: WorkstreamRow): ConflictError | null` (Retry's checks on a failed workstream: its task is parked, it has a newer workstream, or its batch is neither `OPEN` nor `FAILED`) and `requeueAsRetried(ctx: WriteContext, id: WorkstreamId, then: { reason: string; nextAction?: NextAction; patch?: WorkstreamPatch }): WorkstreamDto` (Retry's write: a `FAILED` batch reopens, the current step restarts clean, `attempt`, `recoveryAttempts` and `failureReason` reset, and the workstream goes `QUEUED` with `then.nextAction`, else continue from the step's latest handoff, else start). `retryWorkstream` keeps its signature and uses both. On a `TAKEN_OVER` workstream (a rescued member's failure card still offers Retry) it answers `"<title>" is taken over in a terminal; hand it back, which retries it.`
  - The takeover service takes over a `FAILED` workstream: `takeable` applies `retryRefusal`, and `takeOverFailed` re-reads `FAILED` and `retryRefusal` inside its write, acquires the task's claims (refusing, with the holder named, when another workstream holds one) and enters `TAKEN_OVER` with `rescued: true`, in one write. `handBack` of a rescued workstream checks `retryRefusal` again inside its write and ends with `requeueAsRetried(…, { nextAction: resume, … })` instead of its plain `QUEUED` transition.

`apps/daemon/src/takeover/rescue.test.ts`:

```ts
import { ConflictError, mintId, type WorkstreamId } from '@10db/contracts';
import {
  activeClaims,
  endSession,
  getWorkstream,
  insertSession,
  listBoardColumns,
  markSessionStarted,
  moveTask,
  transitionWorkstream,
} from '@10db/db';
import { afterEach, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { createTakeoverHarness, type TakeoverHarness } from '../../test/takeover-harness';
import { retryWorkstream } from '../team';

const turn = { do: 'turn_complete' as const };
const notice = { do: 'await_message' as const, contains: 'taking this session over' };
const write = (path: string) => ({
  do: 'tool' as const,
  toolName: 'Write',
  input: { file_path: path, content: 'x' },
});
const commit = (opId: string, paths: string[]) => ({
  do: 'mcp' as const,
  tool: 'commit',
  args: { opId, paths, message: `Commit ${opId}` },
});

let h: TakeoverHarness;
afterEach(async () => {
  await h.close();
});
const ws = (id: WorkstreamId) => getWorkstream(h.engine.store.db, id);

/**
 * A workstream that failed after one session began its conversation, as recovery leaves one that
 * crashed past its attempts: its claims released, its counters spent.
 */
function failedAfterASession(title = 'Alpha', areas = ['api']): WorkstreamId {
  const { workstream } = h.engine.addTask(title, areas);
  h.engine.state.write((ctx) => {
    transitionWorkstream(ctx, workstream.id, { to: 'STARTING', fence: null });
    transitionWorkstream(ctx, workstream.id, { to: 'RUNNING', fence: null });
    const s = insertSession(ctx, {
      workstreamId: workstream.id,
      provider: 'claude',
      model: 'sonnet',
      effort: 'medium',
      mode: 'managed',
      mcpTokenHash: mintId('Session'),
    });
    markSessionStarted(ctx, s.id, 'provider-failed', null);
    endSession(ctx, s.id, 'crashed');
    transitionWorkstream(ctx, workstream.id, {
      to: 'FAILED',
      fence: null,
      patch: { failureReason: 'Recovery failed 4 times', recoveryAttempts: 4, attempt: 2 },
    });
  });
  return workstream.id;
}

describe('rescuing a failed workstream', () => {
  it('takes it over with its claims back, and hand back retries it on the same conversation', async () => {
    // Session 1 failed, 2 is the terminal (the fake adapter never runs it), 3 is managed again.
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({
      Alpha: [[], [], [write('src/api/r.txt'), commit('op-rescue-0001', ['src/api/r.txt']), turn]],
    });
    const id = failedAfterASession();
    expect(activeClaims(h.engine.store.db, id)).toEqual([]);

    const taken = await h.takeover.takeOver(id);

    expect(taken).toMatchObject({
      state: 'TAKEN_OVER',
      takeover: { providerSessionId: 'provider-failed', rescued: true, terminal: 'open' },
    });
    expect(activeClaims(h.engine.store.db, id).map((c) => c.name)).toEqual(['api']);
    await h.terminalIs(id, 'open');
    expect(h.claude.lastRun()?.args.slice(0, 2)).toEqual(['--resume', 'provider-failed']);
    await h.quit(id);

    const back = await h.takeover.handBack(id);

    expect(back).toMatchObject({
      state: 'QUEUED',
      takeover: null,
      attempt: 0,
      recoveryAttempts: 0,
      failureReason: null,
      nextAction: { kind: 'resume', providerSessionId: 'provider-failed' },
    });
    h.engine.launch(id);
    await h.engine.runner.idle();
    expect(ws(id).state).toBe('DONE');
    expect(h.engine.adapter.prompts.at(-1)?.resume).toBe('provider-failed');
  });

  it.each([
    {
      name: 'its task is parked',
      setup: (t: TakeoverHarness, id: WorkstreamId) => {
        const parked = getWorkstream(t.engine.store.db, id);
        const icebox = listBoardColumns(t.engine.store.db, parked.projectId)[0];
        if (!icebox) throw new Error('no Icebox');
        t.engine.state.write((ctx) =>
          moveTask(ctx, parked.taskId, { to: icebox.id, after: null, before: null }),
        );
      },
      message: 'is parked; move it to Backlog or Ready before retrying it',
    },
    {
      name: 'another workstream holds its area now',
      setup: async (t: TakeoverHarness) => {
        const { workstream } = t.engine.addTask('Beta', ['api']);
        t.engine.launch(workstream.id);
        await t.running(workstream.id);
      },
      message: '"Beta" holds api now',
    },
  ])('refuses when $name', async ({ setup, message }) => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({ Beta: [[notice, turn]] });
    const id = failedAfterASession();
    await setup(h, id);
    const refused = h.takeover.takeOver(id);
    await expect(refused).rejects.toThrow(ConflictError);
    await expect(refused).rejects.toThrow(message);
    expect(ws(id)).toMatchObject({ state: 'FAILED', takeover: null });
    expect(activeClaims(h.engine.store.db, id)).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('refuses a failed workstream that never began a conversation', async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({});
    const { workstream } = h.engine.addTask('Alpha');
    h.engine.state.write((ctx) => {
      for (const to of ['STARTING', 'FAILED'] as const)
        transitionWorkstream(ctx, workstream.id, { to, fence: null });
    });
    await expect(h.takeover.takeOver(workstream.id)).rejects.toThrow(
      '"Alpha" has not started a Claude session yet',
    );
  });

  it("answers Retry on the rescued workstream's failure card with the way back", async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({});
    const id = failedAfterASession();
    await h.takeover.takeOver(id);

    expect(() => retryWorkstream(h.engine.state, id)).toThrow(
      '"Alpha" is taken over in a terminal; hand it back, which retries it.',
    );
  });

  it('cancels a rescued workstream and frees the claims it took back', async () => {
    h = await createTakeoverHarness({});
    const id = failedAfterASession();
    await h.takeover.takeOver(id);
    await h.quit(id);

    expect(h.takeover.cancel(id)).toMatchObject({ state: 'CANCELLED', takeover: null });
    expect(activeClaims(h.engine.store.db, id)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

```ts
/** A one-task batch whose workstream failed after one session began its conversation. */
async function failedInBatch(): Promise<{ batch: BatchDto; id: WorkstreamId }> {
  const { batch, id } = await parkedInBatch();
  h.settle(id, 'FAILED');

describe('rescuing a failed member', () => {
  it('of an open batch: Ship anyway and Abandon are refused until it is handed back', async () => {
    const { batch, id } = await failedInBatch();

  it('of a batch that shipped: refused, as Retry is', async () => {

      transitionBatch(ctx, batch.id, { to: 'SHIPPING' });
      transitionBatch(ctx, batch.id, { to: 'SHIPPED', patch: { prUrl: 'https://example.com/pr/1' } });
    });
    await expect(takeover.takeOver(id)).rejects.toThrow(
      'Batch "Greetings" is shipped; start the task again to run it in a new batch',
    );
    expect(activeClaims(h.store.db, id)).toEqual([]);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Share Retry's checks and its write**

In `apps/daemon/src/team/steps.ts` (import `type NextAction` from `@10db/contracts` and `type WorkstreamPatch`, `type WorkstreamRow` from `@10db/db` where missing), split `retryWorkstream`:

```ts
/** Why Retry, or a rescue in a terminal, can't take this failed workstream back; null when it can. */
export function retryRefusal(db: DbReader, row: WorkstreamRow): ConflictError | null {
  const task = getTask(db, row.taskId);
  if (task.boardColumnId !== null)
    return new ConflictError(
      `${task.title} is parked; move it to Backlog or Ready before retrying it`,
    );
  if (task.workstreamId !== row.id)
    return new ConflictError(
      `${task.title} has a newer workstream; retry that one or start the task`,
    );
  const batch = row.batchId ? getBatch(db, row.batchId) : null;
  if (batch && batch.state !== BatchState.Open && batch.state !== BatchState.Failed)
    return new ConflictError(
      `Batch "${batch.title}" is ${batch.state.toLowerCase()}; start the task again to run it in a new batch`,
    );
  return null;
}

/**
 * What Retry does to a failed workstream it queues again: a failed batch reopens, the current step
 * restarts clean, the failure count resets, and it continues from `then.nextAction`, else from the
 * step's latest handoff, else from the start. Callers have checked `retryRefusal`.
 */
export function requeueAsRetried(
  ctx: WriteContext,
  id: WorkstreamId,
  then: {
    readonly reason: string;
    readonly nextAction?: NextAction;
    readonly patch?: WorkstreamPatch;
  },
): WorkstreamDto {
  const row = getWorkstreamRow(ctx.tx, id);
  const batch = row.batchId ? getBatch(ctx.tx, row.batchId) : null;
  if (batch?.state === BatchState.Failed)
    transitionBatch(ctx, batch.id, {
      to: BatchState.Open,
      patch: { failure: null },
      reason: 'a workstream was retried',
    });
  const steps = row.steps.length > 0 ? restartStep(row.steps, row.stepIndex, ctx.now) : row.steps;
  const latest = latestHandoffForStep(ctx.tx, { ...row, steps });
  return transitionWorkstream(ctx, id, {
    to: WorkstreamState.Queued,
    fence: null,
    patch: {
      ...then.patch,
      attempt: 0,
      recoveryAttempts: 0,
      failureReason: null,
      steps,
      nextAction:
        then.nextAction ??
        (latest ? { kind: 'continue_from_handoff', handoffId: latest.id } : { kind: 'start' }),
    },
    reason: then.reason,
  });
}

export function retryWorkstream(state: StateStore, id: WorkstreamId): WorkstreamDto {
  const row = getWorkstreamRow(state.db, id);
  // A rescued workstream's failure card still offers Retry: hand-back is its retry.
  if (row.state === WorkstreamState.TakenOver)
    throw new ConflictError(
      `"${getTask(state.db, row.taskId).title}" is taken over in a terminal; hand it back, which retries it.`,
    );
  if (row.state !== WorkstreamState.Failed)
    throw new ConflictError('Only a failed workstream can be retried');
  const refusal = retryRefusal(state.db, row);
  if (refusal) throw refusal;
  return state.write((ctx) => requeueAsRetried(ctx, id, { reason: 'retried by the builder' }));
}
```

In `apps/daemon/src/team/index.ts`, add `requeueAsRetried` and `retryRefusal` to the `./steps` export.

```ts
/** A failed workstream has no runner: it takes its claims back and goes straight to TAKEN_OVER. */
async function takeOverFailed(
  row: WorkstreamRow,
  title: string,
  conversation: { readonly providerSessionId: string; readonly accountId: AccountId },
): Promise<void> {
  const project = getProject(db, row.projectId);
  const head = await deps.git.head(project.repoPath);
  const branch = await deps.git.currentBranch(project.repoPath);
  deps.state.write((ctx) => {
    const now = getWorkstreamRow(ctx.tx, row.id);
    if (now.state !== S.Failed)
      throw new ConflictError(`"${title}" moved on to ${now.state}; try again.`);
    // Parked, superseded or its batch finished while git was read: Retry's own checks, again.
    const refusal = retryRefusal(ctx.tx, now);
    if (refusal) throw refusal;
    // Released when it failed; the builder edits in these areas now, so agents must stay out.
    const claimed = acquireClaims(ctx, {
      projectId: project.id,
      workstreamId: row.id,
      requests: claimRequestsForTask(getTask(ctx.tx, row.taskId), project),
      leaseExpiresAt: ctx.now,
    });
    if (!claimed.ok)
      throw new ConflictError(
        `"${getWorkstream(ctx.tx, claimed.conflict.heldBy).taskTitle}" holds ${claimed.conflict.name} now; take "${title}" over once it is done.`,
      );
    enterTakeover(ctx, row.id, null, {
      ...conversation,
      head,
      branch,
      takenAt: ctx.now,
      eventSeq: latestEventSeq(ctx.tx),
      rescued: true,
      freshFrom: null,
      lastExit: null,
    });
  });
}
```

- in `takeOver`, the runner-less branch becomes:

```ts
if (deps.runner.isRunning(id)) await deps.runner.takeOver(id);
else if (row.state === S.Failed) await takeOverFailed(row, title, conversation);
else await takeOverParked(row, title, conversation);
```

- in `handBack`'s write, replace the final `transitionWorkstream(ctx, id, { to: S.Queued, … })` with:

```ts
const resume: NextAction = {
  kind: 'resume',
  providerSessionId: record.providerSessionId,
  reason: handBackReason(commits.length),
};
const handedBack = { takeover: null, currentSessionId: null } as const;
if (record.rescued) {
  // A rescue is a Retry by hand: Retry's checks hold at the write, the failure count resets and a
  // failed batch reopens.
  const refusal = retryRefusal(ctx.tx, getWorkstreamRow(ctx.tx, id));
  if (refusal) throw refusal;
  requeueAsRetried(ctx, id, {
    reason: 'handed back from the terminal',
    nextAction: resume,
    patch: handedBack,
  });
} else {
  transitionWorkstream(ctx, id, {
    to: S.Queued,
    fence: null,
    patch: { ...handedBack, nextAction: resume },
    reason: 'handed back from the terminal',
  });
}
```

(import `type NextAction` from `@10db/contracts`). A rescue's claim lease is `ctx.now`, as nothing renews a workstream with no lane; the scheduler renews it when it launches the work again, and an active claim is held until it is released whatever its lease says.

### Task 11: Wiring, the takeover routes, the terminal channel and the bundled PTY

**Files:**

- Create: `apps/daemon/src/api/terminal-channel.ts`
- Modify: `apps/daemon/src/api/app.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/api/services.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/daemon/start.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/daemon/services.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/main.ts`, `apps/daemon/.env.example`, `apps/daemon/src/env.ts` (a comment), `apps/daemon/scripts/build.ts`
- Create: `scripts/release/sign-native.sh`; Modify: `scripts/release/build-macos.sh`, `apps/desktop/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json` (`beforeBundleCommand`)
- Test: `apps/daemon/src/api/app.test.ts`, `apps/daemon/src/api/terminal-channel.test.ts` (new), `apps/daemon/test/takeover.test.ts` (new), `apps/daemon/test/bundle.test.ts`, `tests/repo/src/release-scripts.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**

- Consumes: Tasks 4–10; the accounts plan's `launcher` and account runtime in `start.ts`.
- Produces:
  - `ApiServices`: `takeOver(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<WorkstreamDto>`, `handBack(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<WorkstreamDto>`, `attachTerminal(id: WorkstreamId, sink: TerminalSink): TerminalControl | null`, with `TerminalSink` `{ output(data: string): void; exited(exit: TerminalExit): void; close(): void }` and `TerminalControl` `{ input(data: string): void; resize(size: TerminalSize): void; detach(): void }` declared in `api/services.ts` (the API layer imports no daemon module; Task 6's types have the same shape). `cancelWorkstream` sends a `TAKEN_OVER` workstream to `Takeover.cancel`.
  - Routes: `POST /workstreams/:id/takeover` → `200 WorkstreamDto`; `POST /workstreams/:id/hand-back` → `200 WorkstreamDto`; `GET /workstreams/:id/terminal`, a WebSocket upgrade. Refusals answer 409 or 400 with the message.
  - `terminalEvents(services: Pick<ApiServices, 'attachTerminal'>, rawId: string): WSEvents` (`api/terminal-channel.ts`).
  - The auth middleware takes the token from the `token` query parameter on both WebSocket paths (`/events/stream` and `/workstreams/:id/terminal`) and from the `Authorization` header everywhere else, and refuses an upgrade on either from an origin outside `allowedOrigins`.
  - `DaemonOptions.claudePath?: string | null` (the `claude` a terminal runs; absent or null refuses every takeover with 400).
  - The terminal host is built in `start.ts`; shutdown awaits `terminals.stopAll()` while the database is open. Keep-awake and the watcher refresh on `takeover.changed` too.
  - `main.ts`'s `findClaude()`: `TENDB_CLAUDE_PATH` when set (fake sessions included, so the E2E's terminal runs its fake `claude`), else nothing under fake sessions, else the system check's `claude`.
  - The daemon's WebSocket server takes frames of at most `TERMINAL_FRAME_MAX_BYTES` (ws's default is 100 MiB); a larger one closes the socket (1009).
  - The bundle carries `node_modules/@lydell/node-pty-<platform>-<arch>/` beside `daemon.mjs`.
  - A signed release signs `better_sqlite3.node`, `pty.node` and `spawn-helper` with the hardened runtime before Tauri signs the app (`scripts/release/sign-native.sh`, run as Tauri's `beforeBundleCommand`), and `build-macos.sh`'s signed branch verifies them inside the built app. Unsigned builds leave them as they are.
