`apps/daemon/src/api/terminal-channel.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
  mintId,
  TERMINAL_INPUT_MAX,
  TerminalCloseCode,
  type TerminalExit,
  type TerminalSize,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { WSContext } from 'hono/ws';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import type { TerminalSink } from './services';
import { terminalEvents } from './terminal-channel';

/** A socket that records what is sent and how it closed, and a terminal that records what it got. */
function harness(opts: { readonly attached: boolean } = { attached: true }) {
  const sent: unknown[] = [];
  const closed: { code: number | undefined; reason: string | undefined }[] = [];
  const got: { input: string[]; sizes: TerminalSize[]; detached: number } = {
    input: [],
    sizes: [],
    detached: 0,
  };
  let sink: TerminalSink | null = null;
  const ws = new WSContext({
    send: (data) => {
      sent.push(JSON.parse(String(data)));
    },
    close: (code, reason) => {
      closed.push({ code, reason });
    },
    readyState: 1,
  });
  const id = mintId('Workstream');
  const events = terminalEvents(
    {
      attachTerminal: (_id, s) => {
        if (!opts.attached) return null;
        sink = s;
        return {
          input: (data) => {
            got.input.push(data);
          },
          resize: (size) => {
            got.sizes.push(size);
          },
          detach: () => {
            got.detached += 1;
          },
        };
      },
    },
    id,
  );
  events.onOpen?.(new Event('open'), ws);
  const message = (data: string) => {
    events.onMessage?.(new MessageEvent('message', { data }), ws);
  };
  return { sent, closed, got, message, sink: () => sink, events, ws };
}

describe('terminalEvents', () => {
  it('sends keystrokes and sizes to the terminal', () => {
    const t = harness();
    t.message(JSON.stringify({ type: 'input', data: 'ls\r' }));
    t.message(JSON.stringify({ type: 'resize', cols: 90, rows: 30 }));
    expect(t.got).toMatchObject({ input: ['ls\r'], sizes: [{ cols: 90, rows: 30 }] });
    expect(t.closed).toEqual([]);
  });

  it('sends output and the exit to the pane as messages', () => {
    const t = harness();
    const exit: TerminalExit = { reason: 'exited', exitCode: 0, signal: null, message: null };
    t.sink()?.output('hello');
    t.sink()?.exited(exit);
    expect(t.sent).toEqual([
      { type: 'output', data: 'hello' },
      { type: 'exit', exit },
    ]);
  });

  it.each([
    { name: 'text that is not JSON', data: '{nope' },
    { name: 'an unknown message', data: JSON.stringify({ type: 'kill' }) },
    {
      name: 'an input over the limit',
      data: JSON.stringify({ type: 'input', data: 'x'.repeat(TERMINAL_INPUT_MAX + 1) }),
    },
    { name: 'a size past the limits', data: JSON.stringify({ type: 'resize', cols: 5000, rows: 30 }) },
  ])('closes the socket on $name, and forwards nothing', ({ data }) => {
    const t = harness();
    t.message(data);
    expect(t.closed).toEqual([{ code: TerminalCloseCode.BadMessage, reason: 'Not a terminal message' }]);
    expect(t.got).toMatchObject({ input: [], sizes: [] });
  });

  it('closes the socket when the workstream has no terminal', () => {
    const t = harness({ attached: false });
    expect(t.closed).toEqual([
      { code: TerminalCloseCode.NoTerminal, reason: 'No terminal for this workstream' },
    ]);
  });

  it('detaches when the pane goes', () => {
    const t = harness();
    t.events.onClose?.(new CloseEvent('close'), t.ws);
    expect(t.got.detached).toBe(1);
  });
});
```

`apps/daemon/test/takeover.test.ts`:

```ts
import {
  ProjectDto,
  TaskDto,
  TERMINAL_FRAME_MAX_BYTES,
  TerminalCloseCode,
  TerminalServerMessage,
  WorkstreamDto,
  type WorkstreamId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { latestConversation } from '@10db/db';
import { afterAll, beforeAll, describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { WebSocket } from 'ws';
import { z } from 'zod';
import { startDaemon, type RunningDaemon } from '../src/daemon';
import { FakeAdapter, type FakeScriptBook } from '../src/sessions';
import { apiClient, quickFlowId } from './api-client';
import { createFakeClaude, type FakeClaude } from './fake-claude';
import { createTempRepo, tempDir, waitFor, waitForAsync, type TempDir } from './fixtures';

const turn = { do: 'turn_complete' as const };
/** Session 1 is managed, 2 is the terminal (the fake adapter never runs it), 3 is managed again. */
const BOOK: FakeScriptBook = {
  Alpha: [
    [{ do: 'await_message', contains: 'taking this session over' }, turn, { do: 'hang' }],
    [],
    [
      { do: 'tool', toolName: 'Write', input: { file_path: 'src/a/one.txt', content: 'x' } },
      {
        do: 'mcp',
        tool: 'commit',
        args: { opId: 'op-take-0001', paths: ['src/a/one.txt'], message: 'Add one' },
      },
      turn,
    ],
  ],
};

let daemon: RunningDaemon;
let repo: TempDir;
let data: TempDir;
let claude: FakeClaude;
let api: ReturnType<typeof apiClient>;

beforeAll(async () => {
  repo = createTempRepo();
  data = tempDir('takeover');
  claude = createFakeClaude();
  daemon = await startDaemon({
    dataDir: data.path,
    port: 0,
    adapter: new FakeAdapter(BOOK),
    logLevel: 'warn',
    caffeinate: () => null,
    watch: false,
    claudePath: claude.path,
  });
  api = apiClient(daemon.url, daemon.token);
});
afterAll(async () => {
  await daemon.stop();
  claude.cleanup();
  repo.cleanup();
  data.cleanup();
});

const workstream = (id: WorkstreamId) =>
  api
    .get('/workstreams?active=false', z.array(WorkstreamDto))
    .then((all) => all.find((w) => w.id === id));

/** A pane's socket: what the daemon sent, and how it closed. */
function socket(id: WorkstreamId, token: string | null = daemon.token) {
  const url = new URL(`/workstreams/${id}/terminal`, daemon.url.replace('http', 'ws'));
  if (token !== null) url.searchParams.set('token', token);
  const client = new WebSocket(url);
  const messages: TerminalServerMessage[] = [];
  let status: number | null = null;
  let closedWith: number | null = null;
  client.on('message', (raw: Buffer) => {
    messages.push(TerminalServerMessage.parse(JSON.parse(raw.toString())));
  });
  client.on('unexpected-response', (_req, res) => {
    status = res.statusCode ?? null;
  });
  client.on('error', () => undefined);
  client.on('close', (code) => {
    closedWith = code;
  });
  return {
    text: () =>
      messages.map((m) => (m.type === 'output' ? m.data : '')).join(''),
    exits: () => messages.flatMap((m) => (m.type === 'exit' ? [m.exit] : [])),
    status: () => status,
    closedWith: () => closedWith,
    opened: () =>
      new Promise<void>((resolve, reject) => {
        client.once('open', () => {
          resolve();
        });
        client.once('error', reject);
      }),
    send: (message: unknown) => {
      client.send(JSON.stringify(message));
    },
    sendRaw: (data: string) => {
      client.send(data);
    },
    close: () => {
      client.close();
    },
  };
}

describe('takeover over HTTP and the terminal channel', () => {
  it('takes a session over, drives its terminal over the WebSocket, reattaches, and finishes it in managed mode', async () => {
    const project = await api.post('/projects', ProjectDto, {
      name: 'takeover',
      repoPath: repo.path,
      gateCommand: 'test -d src',
      flowId: await quickFlowId(api),
      areas: [{ name: 'a', globs: ['src/a/**'] }],
    });
    const task = await api.post(`/projects/${project.id}/tasks`, TaskDto, {
      title: 'Alpha',
      acceptanceCriteria: ['src/a/one.txt exists'],
      areas: ['a'],
    });
    const started = await api.post(`/tasks/${task.id}/start`, WorkstreamDto);
    await waitForAsync(async () => {
      const w = await workstream(started.id);
      return w?.state === 'RUNNING' && latestConversation(daemon.state.db, started.id) !== null;
    });

    expect((await api.post(`/workstreams/${started.id}/takeover`, WorkstreamDto)).state).toBe(
      'TAKEN_OVER',
    );

    // No token, no terminal.
    const stranger = socket(started.id, null);
    await waitFor(() => stranger.status() ?? undefined);
    expect(stranger.status()).toBe(401);

    const pane = socket(started.id);
    await pane.opened();
    await waitFor(() => pane.text().includes('fake claude: ready'));
    pane.send({ type: 'resize', cols: 90, rows: 30 });
    pane.send({ type: 'input', data: 'size\r' });
    await waitFor(() => pane.text().includes('30 90'));
    pane.send({ type: 'input', data: 'hello\r' });
    await waitFor(() => pane.text().includes('you said: hello'));
    pane.close();
    await waitFor(() => pane.closedWith() ?? undefined);

    // A frame past the server's limit closes that socket before any parsing.
    const flood = socket(started.id);
    await flood.opened();
    flood.sendRaw('x'.repeat(TERMINAL_FRAME_MAX_BYTES + 1));
    await waitFor(() => flood.closedWith() ?? undefined);
    expect(flood.closedWith()).toBe(1009);

    // The pane went; Claude did not.
    expect((await workstream(started.id))?.takeover?.terminal).toBe('open');
    const again = socket(started.id);
    await again.opened();
    await waitFor(() => again.text().includes('you said: hello'));
    await expect(api.post(`/workstreams/${started.id}/hand-back`, WorkstreamDto)).rejects.toThrow(
      '-> 409',
    );
    again.send({ type: 'input', data: '/exit\r' });
    await waitFor(() => again.exits()[0]);
    expect(again.exits()).toEqual([{ reason: 'exited', exitCode: 0, signal: null, message: null }]);
    await waitForAsync(async () => (await workstream(started.id))?.takeover?.terminal === 'closed');

    expect((await api.post(`/workstreams/${started.id}/hand-back`, WorkstreamDto)).state).toBe(
      'QUEUED',
    );
    // Hand back dropped the scrollback and closed the pane still attached.
    await waitFor(() => again.closedWith() ?? undefined);
    const late = socket(started.id);
    await waitFor(() => late.closedWith() ?? undefined);
    expect(late.closedWith()).toBe(TerminalCloseCode.NoTerminal);
    const done = await waitForAsync(async () => {
      const w = await workstream(started.id);
      return w?.state === 'DONE' ? w : undefined;
    }, 30_000);
    expect(done.sessionCount).toBe(3);
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Add the services, the channel and the routes**

In `apps/daemon/src/api/services.ts`, import `type TerminalExit` and `type TerminalSize` from `@10db/contracts` and add:

```ts
/** A pane's socket: the terminal's output and its end go out through it. */
export interface TerminalSink {
  output(data: string): void;
  exited(exit: TerminalExit): void;
  close(): void;
}

/** What a pane's socket does to the terminal it is attached to. */
export interface TerminalControl {
  input(data: string): void;
  resize(size: TerminalSize): void;
  detach(): void;
}
```

and to `ApiServices`:

```ts
  /** Pauses the session at a safe point and opens it in a daemon terminal; on a closed terminal, opens it again (spec §5.4). */
  takeOver(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<WorkstreamDto>;
  /** Reconciles git and resumes the conversation in managed mode. */
  handBack(id: WorkstreamId): Promise<WorkstreamDto>;
  /** Connects a pane's socket to the workstream's terminal; null when 10db has no terminal for it this run. */
  attachTerminal(id: WorkstreamId, sink: TerminalSink): TerminalControl | null;
```

`apps/daemon/src/api/terminal-channel.ts`:

```ts
import {
  TerminalClientMessage,
  TerminalCloseCode,
  WorkstreamId,
  type TerminalServerMessage,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import type { WSContext, WSEvents } from 'hono/ws';
import type { ApiServices, TerminalControl } from './services';

function send(ws: WSContext, message: TerminalServerMessage): void {
  ws.send(JSON.stringify(message));
}

/** A pane's message, or null for anything else: text that isn't JSON, an unknown type, a value past its limits. */
function parse(data: unknown): TerminalClientMessage | null {
  if (typeof data !== 'string') return null;
  let json: unknown;
  try {
    json = JSON.parse(data);
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
  const parsed = TerminalClientMessage.safeParse(json);
  return parsed.success ? parsed.data : null;
}

/**
 * One pane's socket on a workstream's terminal (spec §5.4). Keystrokes reach a shell, so every message
 * is parsed at this boundary and a bad one closes the socket.
 */
export function terminalEvents(
  services: Pick<ApiServices, 'attachTerminal'>,
  rawId: string,
): WSEvents {
  let control: TerminalControl | null = null;
  return {
    onOpen(_event, ws) {
      const id = WorkstreamId.safeParse(rawId);
      control = id.success
        ? services.attachTerminal(id.data, {
            output: (data) => {
              send(ws, { type: 'output', data });
            },
            exited: (exit) => {
              send(ws, { type: 'exit', exit });
            },
            close: () => {
              ws.close(1000, 'The terminal was handed back or cancelled');
            },
          })
        : null;
      if (!control) ws.close(TerminalCloseCode.NoTerminal, 'No terminal for this workstream');
    },
    onMessage(event, ws) {
      const message = parse(event.data);
      if (!message) {
        ws.close(TerminalCloseCode.BadMessage, 'Not a terminal message');
        return;
      }
      switch (message.type) {
        case 'input':
          control?.input(message.data);
          return;
        case 'resize':
          control?.resize({ cols: message.cols, rows: message.rows });
          return;
      }
    },
    onClose() {
      control?.detach();
      control = null;
    },
  };
}
```

```ts
const TERMINAL_PATH = /^\/workstreams\/[^/]+\/terminal$/;

/** WebSocket routes: a browser can't set a header on an upgrade, so these take the token in the query. */
function isSocketPath(path: string): boolean {
  return path === STREAM_PATH || TERMINAL_PATH.test(path);
}
```

- and add:

```ts
/**
 * @lydell/node-pty requires its platform package by name (`pty.node`, and `spawn-helper` on macOS),
 * so the bundle carries that one package in a node_modules folder of its own. cpSync keeps
 * spawn-helper's execute bit.
 */
function ptyPlatformPackage(): { dir: string; name: string } {
  const daemonRequire = createRequire(resolve(root, 'package.json'));
  const ptyDir = dirname(daemonRequire.resolve('@lydell/node-pty/package.json'));
  const name = `node-pty-${process.platform}-${process.arch}`;
  const ptyRequire = createRequire(resolve(ptyDir, 'package.json'));
  return { dir: dirname(ptyRequire.resolve(`@lydell/${name}/package.json`)), name };
}
```

- [ ] **Step 7: Sign the native files in a signed release**

`codesign --verify --deep --strict` (`build-macos.sh` line 83) treats a Mach-O file under `Contents/Resources` as data, so it passes an unsigned `spawn-helper`, `pty.node` or `better_sqlite3.node`; notarization rejects them, and macOS refuses to run an unsigned one that carries a quarantine attribute. Tauri signs only the app's executables, so a signed release must sign these three itself, after `pnpm sidecar` copies them and before Tauri bundles and signs the app: Tauri's `beforeBundleCommand`. Apple signing isn't set up yet (the release PR waits on it), so no signed run gates this PR; the first signed release runs both checks below and fails loudly.

Add to `tests/repo/src/release-scripts.test.ts` (import `chmodSync`, `mkdirSync` and `readFileSync` from `node:fs` beside what it has; it already has `run`, `repoRoot`, `readRepoFile`, `mkdtempSync` and `tmpdir`):

```ts
describe('sign-native.sh', () => {
  const script = join(repoRoot, 'scripts', 'release', 'sign-native.sh');

  /** A daemon resources folder holding the three native files, and a codesign that records its arguments. */
  function fixture(): { dir: string; log: string; bin: string } {
    const root = mkdtempSync(join(tmpdir(), 'sign-native-'));
    const daemon = join(root, 'daemon');
    const pty = join(daemon, 'node_modules', '@lydell', 'node-pty-darwin-arm64');
    mkdirSync(pty, { recursive: true });
    for (const file of [join(daemon, 'better_sqlite3.node'), join(pty, 'pty.node'), join(pty, 'spawn-helper')])
      writeFileSync(file, '');
    const bin = join(root, 'bin');
    mkdirSync(bin);
    const log = join(root, 'codesign.log');
    writeFileSync(join(bin, 'codesign'), `#!/bin/sh\necho "$*" >> '${log}'\n`);
    chmodSync(join(bin, 'codesign'), 0o755);
    return { dir: daemon, log, bin };
  }

  it('leaves the native files unsigned, and says so, when no identity is set', () => {
    const f = fixture();
    const result = run('bash', [script], { PATH: `${f.bin}:/usr/bin:/bin`, TENDB_NATIVE_DIR: f.dir });
    expect(result.status).toBe(0);
    expect(result.stdout).toContain('stay unsigned');
  });

  it('signs each native file with the hardened runtime, then verifies it', () => {
    const f = fixture();
    const result = run('bash', [script], {
      PATH: `${f.bin}:/usr/bin:/bin`,
      TENDB_NATIVE_DIR: f.dir,
      TENDB_NATIVE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: 'Developer ID Application: Test (TEAM123)',
      TENDB_NATIVE_SIGNING_KEYCHAIN: '/tmp/native.keychain-db',
    });
    expect(result.status).toBe(0);
    const calls = readFileSync(f.log, 'utf8').trim().split('\n');
    expect(calls).toHaveLength(6);
    for (const name of ['better_sqlite3.node', 'pty.node', 'spawn-helper']) {
      expect(calls).toContainEqual(
        expect.stringMatching(
          new RegExp(`^--force --options runtime --timestamp --keychain /tmp/native.keychain-db -s Developer ID Application: Test \\(TEAM123\\) .*${name}$`),
        ),
      );
      expect(calls).toContainEqual(expect.stringMatching(new RegExp(`^--verify --strict --verbose=2 .*${name}$`)));
    }
  });

  it('fails when a native file is missing', () => {
    const f = fixture();
    const result = run('bash', [script], {
      PATH: `${f.bin}:/usr/bin:/bin`,
      TENDB_NATIVE_DIR: join(f.dir, 'node_modules'),
      TENDB_NATIVE_SIGNING_IDENTITY: 'x',
      TENDB_NATIVE_SIGNING_KEYCHAIN: 'k',
    });
    expect(result.status).not.toBe(0);
    expect(result.stderr).toContain('expected 3 native files');
  });

  it("runs as Tauri's beforeBundleCommand", () => {
    expect(readRepoFile('apps/desktop/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json')).toContain(
      '"beforeBundleCommand": "bash ../../scripts/release/sign-native.sh"',
    );
  });
});
```

Expected: FAIL: `sign-native.sh` doesn't exist (`bash` exits 127), and the config has no `beforeBundleCommand`.

`scripts/release/sign-native.sh` (mode 755):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# Tauri's beforeBundleCommand. Signs the daemon's native files in the sidecar resources with the
# hardened runtime before Tauri bundles and signs the app: codesign --verify --deep treats a Mach-O
# file under Contents/Resources as data, so an unsigned one would pass it and then fail notarization.
# A build with no identity (a dry run, a local build) leaves them as they are.
set -euo pipefail

root="$(cd "$(dirname "$0")/../.." && pwd)"
daemon="${TENDB_NATIVE_DIR:-$root/apps/desktop/src-tauri/resources/daemon}"
identity="${TENDB_NATIVE_SIGNING_IDENTITY:-}"
if [ -z "$identity" ]; then
  echo "sign-native.sh: no signing identity, so the native files stay unsigned"
  exit 0
fi
shopt -s nullglob
files=(
  "$daemon"/better_sqlite3.node
  "$daemon"/node_modules/@lydell/node-pty-darwin-*/pty.node
  "$daemon"/node_modules/@lydell/node-pty-darwin-*/spawn-helper
)
if [ "${#files[@]}" -ne 3 ]; then
  echo "sign-native.sh: expected 3 native files in $daemon, found ${#files[@]}" >&2
  exit 1
fi
for file in "${files[@]}"; do
  codesign --force --options runtime --timestamp --keychain "${TENDB_NATIVE_SIGNING_KEYCHAIN:?}" -s "$identity" "$file"
  codesign --verify --strict --verbose=2 "$file"
done
```

In `apps/desktop/src-tauri/tauri.conf.json`, add `"beforeBundleCommand": "bash ../../scripts/release/sign-native.sh"` to `build` (Tauri runs it from `apps/desktop`, as it runs `beforeBuildCommand`).

In `scripts/release/build-macos.sh`'s signed branch, before `tauri build`, put the Developer ID certificate in a keychain of its own and hand its identity to `sign-native.sh` (Tauri imports `APPLE_CERTIFICATE` only when it signs the app, after `beforeBundleCommand`):

```bash
if [ "$mode" = signed ]; then
  keychain="$(mktemp -d)/native-signing.keychain-db"
  certificate="$(mktemp)"
  printf '%s' "$APPLE_CERTIFICATE" | base64 --decode > "$certificate"
  security create-keychain -p '' "$keychain"
  security set-keychain-settings -lut 3600 "$keychain"
  security unlock-keychain -p '' "$keychain"
  security import "$certificate" -k "$keychain" -P "$APPLE_CERTIFICATE_PASSWORD" -T /usr/bin/codesign
  rm -f "$certificate"
  security set-key-partition-list -S apple-tool:,apple: -s -k '' "$keychain" > /dev/null
  identity="$(security find-identity -v -p codesigning "$keychain" | awk -F'"' '/Developer ID Application/ { print $2; exit }')"
  [ -n "$identity" ] || fail "APPLE_CERTIFICATE holds no Developer ID Application identity"
  export TENDB_NATIVE_SIGNING_IDENTITY="$identity" TENDB_NATIVE_SIGNING_KEYCHAIN="$keychain"
fi
```

and, in the signed branch after the app is mounted (beside its `codesign --verify --deep --strict`), check the three files themselves:

```bash
  daemon="$app/Contents/Resources/daemon"
  for native in "$daemon/better_sqlite3.node" "$daemon"/node_modules/@lydell/node-pty-darwin-arm64/pty.node "$daemon"/node_modules/@lydell/node-pty-darwin-arm64/spawn-helper; do
    codesign --verify --strict --verbose=2 "$native" || fail "$native is not signed"
    [ "$(team_of "$native")" = "$APPLE_TEAM_ID" ] || fail "$(basename "$native") is not signed by team $APPLE_TEAM_ID"
  done
```

```ts
import type { TerminalSize } from '@10db/contracts';
import { createContext } from 'react';
import { createXtermScreen } from './xterm-screen';

/** What the pane draws on: xterm.js in the app, a recorder in jsdom, which has no layout to render it. */
export interface TerminalScreen {
  write(data: string): void;
  /** Keystrokes and pastes; returns the unsubscribe. */
  onInput(listener: (data: string) => void): () => void;
  /** The screen's size whenever it changes; returns the unsubscribe. */
  onResize(listener: (size: TerminalSize) => void): () => void;
  size(): TerminalSize;
  focus(): void;
  dispose(): void;
}

export type ScreenFactory = (element: HTMLElement) => TerminalScreen;

export const ScreenFactoryContext = createContext<ScreenFactory>(createXtermScreen);
```

`apps/desktop/src/test/terminal-screen.ts`:

```ts
import type { TerminalSize } from '@10db/contracts';
import type { ScreenFactory, TerminalScreen } from '../views/terminal/terminal-screen';

/** A screen that records what it is sent, and types and resizes as a builder would. */
export interface RecordingScreen {
  readonly screen: TerminalScreen;
  written(): string;
  type(data: string): void;
  resizeTo(size: TerminalSize): void;
}

export function createRecordingScreen(
  initial: TerminalSize = { cols: 100, rows: 40 },
): RecordingScreen {
  let text = '';
  let size = initial;
  const inputs = new Set<(data: string) => void>();
  const resizes = new Set<(size: TerminalSize) => void>();
  return {
    screen: {
      write: (data) => {
        text += data;
      },
      onInput(listener) {
        inputs.add(listener);
        return () => {
          inputs.delete(listener);
        };
      },
      onResize(listener) {
        resizes.add(listener);
        return () => {
          resizes.delete(listener);
        };
      },
      size: () => size,
      focus: () => undefined,
      dispose: () => undefined,
    },
    written: () => text,
    type(data) {
      for (const listener of inputs) listener(data);
    },
    resizeTo(next) {
      size = next;
      for (const listener of resizes) listener(next);
    },
  };
}

/** A factory for the app in tests: every pane gets a recording screen; `last()` is the newest. */
export function createRecordingScreens(): {
  readonly factory: ScreenFactory;
  last(): RecordingScreen | undefined;
  /** How many screens the app made: one per pane while it stays mounted. */
  count(): number;
} {
  const made: RecordingScreen[] = [];
  return {
    factory: () => {
      const recording = createRecordingScreen();
      made.push(recording);
      return recording.screen;
    },
    last: () => made.at(-1),
    count: () => made.length,
  };
}
```

```ts
import { WorkstreamState as S, type TakeoverDto, type WorkstreamState } from '@10db/contracts';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { takeoverDto } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { takeoverActions, type TakeoverActions } from './takeover-actions';

const NONE: TakeoverActions = { open: null, show: false, handBack: null, note: null };
const TAKE: TakeoverActions = { open: 'take_over', show: false, handBack: null, note: null };
const closed = (note: string): TakeoverActions => ({
  open: 'reopen',
  show: true,
  handBack: 'enabled',
  note,
});

const rows: {
  name: string;
  state: WorkstreamState;
  takeover: TakeoverDto | null;
  expected: TakeoverActions;
}[] = [
  { name: 'running', state: S.Running, takeover: null, expected: TAKE },
  { name: 'handing off', state: S.HandingOff, takeover: null, expected: TAKE },
  { name: 'waiting on the builder', state: S.WaitingHuman, takeover: null, expected: TAKE },
  { name: 'failed, to rescue', state: S.Failed, takeover: null, expected: TAKE },
  { name: 'queued', state: S.Queued, takeover: null, expected: NONE },
  { name: 'verifying', state: S.Verifying, takeover: null, expected: NONE },
  { name: 'done', state: S.Done, takeover: null, expected: NONE },
  {
    name: 'taken over, Claude running',
    state: S.TakenOver,
    takeover: takeoverDto({ terminal: 'open', terminalPid: 4242 }),
    expected: {
      open: null,
      show: true,
      handBack: 'disabled',
      note: 'Claude is running in the terminal (process 4242). Quit it with /exit, then hand back.',
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'taken over, Claude starting',
    state: S.TakenOver,
    takeover: takeoverDto({ terminal: 'open', terminalPid: null }),
    expected: {
      open: null,
      show: true,
      handBack: 'disabled',
      note: 'Claude is running in the terminal. Quit it with /exit, then hand back.',
    },
  },
  {
    name: 'taken over, no terminal yet',
    state: S.TakenOver,
    takeover: takeoverDto(),
    expected: closed('The terminal is closed. Hand back when you are done.'),
  },
  {
    name: 'taken over, Claude exited',
    state: S.TakenOver,
    takeover: takeoverDto({ lastExit: { reason: 'exited', exitCode: 0, signal: null, message: null } }),
    expected: closed('Claude exited with status 0. Hand back when you are done.'),
  },
  {
    name: 'taken over, Claude ended by a signal',
    state: S.TakenOver,
    takeover: takeoverDto({ lastExit: { reason: 'exited', exitCode: null, signal: 9, message: null } }),
    expected: closed('Claude was ended by signal 9. Hand back when you are done.'),
  },
  {
    name: 'taken over, the terminal could not start',
    state: S.TakenOver,
    takeover: takeoverDto({
      lastExit: { reason: 'spawn_failed', exitCode: null, signal: null, message: 'posix_spawnp failed.' },
    }),
    expected: closed('The terminal could not start: posix_spawnp failed. Open it again, or hand back.'),
  },
  {
    name: 'taken over, 10db restarted',
    state: S.TakenOver,
    takeover: takeoverDto({ lastExit: { reason: 'daemon_restart', exitCode: null, signal: null, message: null } }),
    expected: closed(
      'The terminal ended when 10db stopped. Open it again to resume the conversation, or hand back.',
    ),
  },
];

describe('takeoverActions', () => {
  it.each(rows)('$name', ({ state, takeover, expected }) => {
    expect(takeoverActions({ state, takeover })).toEqual(expected);
  });
});
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/terminal/terminal-channel.test.tsx`:

```tsx
import { mintId, TERMINAL_INPUT_MAX, TerminalCloseCode, type WorkstreamId } from '@10db/contracts';
import { screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { baseHandlers, renderWithDaemon, server, terminalSocket } from '../../test/daemon';
import { createRecordingScreen, type RecordingScreen } from '../../test/terminal-screen';
import { useTerminalChannel } from './terminal-channel';

function Channel({ id, recording }: { readonly id: WorkstreamId; readonly recording: RecordingScreen }) {
  return <p data-testid="state">{useTerminalChannel(id, recording.screen)}</p>;
}

/** The daemon's side of the socket: the URLs it was opened with and the messages it got. */
function daemonSide(onOpen: (client: { send(data: string): void; close(code: number, reason: string): void }) => void) {
  const urls: string[] = [];
  const received: unknown[] = [];
  server.use(
    ...baseHandlers(),
    terminalSocket.addEventListener('connection', ({ client }) => {
      urls.push(client.url.toString());
      client.addEventListener('message', (event) => {
        received.push(JSON.parse(String(event.data)));
      });
      onOpen(client);
    }),
  );
  return { urls, received };
}

describe('useTerminalChannel', () => {
  it('attaches with the token, sends its size first, and writes the output it gets', async () => {
    const id = mintId('Workstream');
    const recording = createRecordingScreen({ cols: 100, rows: 40 });
    const side = daemonSide((client) => {
      client.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'output', data: 'fake claude: ready' }));
    });
    renderWithDaemon(<Channel id={id} recording={recording} />);

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(recording.written()).toBe('fake claude: ready');
    });
    const url = new URL(side.urls[0] ?? 'ws://missing');
    expect(url.pathname).toBe(`/workstreams/${id}/terminal`);
    expect(url.searchParams.get('token')).toBe('test-token');
    expect(side.received[0]).toEqual({ type: 'resize', cols: 100, rows: 40 });
    expect(screen.getByTestId('state').textContent).toBe('attached');
  });

  it('sends keystrokes, a large paste in pieces, and new sizes', async () => {
    const recording = createRecordingScreen();
    const side = daemonSide(() => undefined);
    renderWithDaemon(<Channel id={mintId('Workstream')} recording={recording} />);
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(side.received).toHaveLength(1);
    });

    recording.type('ls\r');
    recording.type('x'.repeat(TERMINAL_INPUT_MAX + 10));
    recording.resizeTo({ cols: 80, rows: 24 });

    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(side.received.slice(1)).toEqual([
        { type: 'input', data: 'ls\r' },
        { type: 'input', data: 'x'.repeat(TERMINAL_INPUT_MAX) },
        { type: 'input', data: 'x'.repeat(10) },
        { type: 'resize', cols: 80, rows: 24 },
      ]);
    });
  });

  it('says when the workstream has no terminal', async () => {
    daemonSide((client) => {
      client.close(TerminalCloseCode.NoTerminal, 'No terminal for this workstream');
    });
    renderWithDaemon(<Channel id={mintId('Workstream')} recording={createRecordingScreen()} />);
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screen.getByTestId('state').textContent).toBe('no_terminal');
    });
  });
});
```

```ts
import { fireEvent, screen, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react';
import { http, HttpResponse } from 'msw';
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { baseHandlers, daemonUrl, renderApp, server, terminalSocket } from '../../test/daemon';
import { takeoverDto, workstreamDto } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { createRecordingScreens } from '../../test/terminal-screen';

describe('TerminalView', () => {
  it('shows a running terminal: its output, its note, and Hand back held back', async () => {
    let connections = 0;
    const ws = workstreamDto({
      taskTitle: 'Alpha',
      state: 'TAKEN_OVER',
      takeover: takeoverDto({ terminal: 'open', terminalPid: 4242 }),
    });
    server.use(
      ...baseHandlers(),
      http.get(daemonUrl('/workstreams'), () => HttpResponse.json([ws])),
      terminalSocket.addEventListener('connection', ({ client }) => {
        connections += 1;
        client.send(JSON.stringify({ type: 'output', data: 'fake claude: ready' }));
      }),
    );
    const screens = createRecordingScreens();
    renderApp(`/terminal/${ws.id}`, { screens: screens.factory });

    expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Terminal: Alpha', level: 2 })).toBeTruthy();
    expect(
      screen.getByText(
        'Claude is running in the terminal (process 4242). Quit it with /exit, then hand back.',
      ),
    ).toBeTruthy();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hand back' }).hasAttribute('disabled')).toBe(true);
    expect(screen.queryByRole('button', { name: 'Show terminal' })).toBeNull();
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(screens.last()?.written()).toBe('fake claude: ready');
    });
    // The note and the buttons re-render as queries settle; the pane keeps its one screen and socket.
    expect(screens.count()).toBe(1);
    expect(connections).toBe(1);
  });

  it('opens a closed terminal again, and hands it back', async () => {
    const ws = workstreamDto({
      taskTitle: 'Alpha',
      state: 'TAKEN_OVER',
      takeover: takeoverDto({ lastExit: { reason: 'exited', exitCode: 0, signal: null, message: null } }),
    });
    const calls: string[] = [];
    server.use(
      ...baseHandlers(),
      http.get(daemonUrl('/workstreams'), () => HttpResponse.json([ws])),
      http.post(daemonUrl('/workstreams/:id/takeover'), ({ params }) => {
        calls.push(`take ${String(params['id'])}`);
        return HttpResponse.json({ ...ws, takeover: takeoverDto({ terminal: 'open' }) });
      }),
      http.post(daemonUrl('/workstreams/:id/hand-back'), ({ params }) => {
        calls.push(`back ${String(params['id'])}`);
        return HttpResponse.json({ ...ws, state: 'QUEUED', takeover: null });
      }),
    );
    renderApp(`/terminal/${ws.id}`);

    expect(await screen.findByText('Claude exited with status 0. Hand back when you are done.')).toBeTruthy();
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open terminal again' }));
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(calls).toEqual([`take ${ws.id}`]);
    });
    fireEvent.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hand back' }));
    await waitFor(() => {
      expect(calls).toEqual([`take ${ws.id}`, `back ${ws.id}`]);
    });
  });
});
```

In `apps/desktop/src/daemon/queries.ts`:

```ts
export function useTakeOver() {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: WorkstreamId) =>
      readJson(await client.workstreams[':id'].takeover.$post({ param: { id } }), WorkstreamDto),
    onSuccess: () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['workstreams'] }),
  });
}

export function useHandBack() {
  const { client } = useDaemon();
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return useMutation({
    mutationFn: async (id: WorkstreamId) =>
      readJson(
        await client.workstreams[':id']['hand-back'].$post({ param: { id } }),
        WorkstreamDto,
      ),
    onSuccess: () =>
      Promise.all([
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['workstreams'] }),
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: ['tasks'] }),
      ]),
  });
}
```

`takeover.changed` events carry `entityType: 'workstream'`, which already refreshes the workstream lists (`INVALIDATIONS`).

`apps/desktop/src/views/terminal/takeover-actions.ts`:

```ts
import {
  TAKEABLE_STATES,
  TerminalStatus,
  WorkstreamState,
  type TerminalExit,
  type WorkstreamDto,
} from '@10db/contracts';

export interface TakeoverActions {
  /** Take managed or failed work over, or open a taken-over one's closed terminal again. */
  readonly open: 'take_over' | 'reopen' | null;
  /** Go to the terminal view; offered while the workstream is taken over. */
  readonly show: boolean;
  /** Offered while taken over; disabled while Claude runs in the terminal. */
  readonly handBack: 'enabled' | 'disabled' | null;
  /** One line about the terminal, for the Factory row, the board card and the terminal view. */
  readonly note: string | null;
}

export const OPEN_LABELS: Readonly<Record<'take_over' | 'reopen', string>> = {
  take_over: 'Open in terminal',
  reopen: 'Open terminal again',
};
export const SHOW_TERMINAL = 'Show terminal';

const NONE: TakeoverActions = { open: null, show: false, handBack: null, note: null };

/** How the terminal ended, in a line (spec §5.4: no card; the row says it). */
function closedNote(exit: TerminalExit | null): string {
  if (exit === null) return 'The terminal is closed. Hand back when you are done.';
  switch (exit.reason) {
    case 'exited':
      return exit.signal === null
        ? `Claude exited${exit.exitCode === null ? '' : ` with status ${String(exit.exitCode)}`}. Hand back when you are done.`
        : `Claude was ended by signal ${String(exit.signal)}. Hand back when you are done.`;
    case 'spawn_failed':
      return `The terminal could not start: ${exit.message ?? 'no reason given.'} Open it again, or hand back.`;
    case 'daemon_shutdown':
    case 'daemon_restart':
      return 'The terminal ended when 10db stopped. Open it again to resume the conversation, or hand back.';
  }
}

/** What the builder can do about a workstream and its terminal (spec §5.4, §10). */
export function takeoverActions(ws: Pick<WorkstreamDto, 'state' | 'takeover'>): TakeoverActions {
  if (TAKEABLE_STATES.includes(ws.state)) return { ...NONE, open: 'take_over' };
  if (ws.state !== WorkstreamState.TakenOver || !ws.takeover) return NONE;
  switch (ws.takeover.terminal) {
    case TerminalStatus.Open:
      return {
        open: null,
        show: true,
        handBack: 'disabled',
        note: `Claude is running in the terminal${ws.takeover.terminalPid === null ? '' : ` (process ${String(ws.takeover.terminalPid)})`}. Quit it with /exit, then hand back.`,
      };
    case TerminalStatus.Closed:
      return { open: 'reopen', show: true, handBack: 'enabled', note: closedNote(ws.takeover.lastExit) };
  }
}
```

(The `spawn_failed` note joins the PTY's message, which ends with its own full stop, to "Open it again"; the test row pins the result.)

`apps/desktop/src/views/terminal/takeover-modal.tsx`:

```tsx
import { WorkstreamState, type WorkstreamDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import { Alert, Button, Group, List, Modal, Stack, Text } from '@mantine/core';

export interface TakeoverModalProps {
  readonly workstream: WorkstreamDto | null;
  readonly loading: boolean;
  readonly error: Error | null;
  readonly onClose: () => void;
  readonly onConfirm: () => void;
}

/** Confirms a takeover and says the rules of the shared checkout while the guard is off (spec §5.4). */
export function TakeoverModal({ workstream, loading, error, onClose, onConfirm }: TakeoverModalProps) {
  const title = workstream?.taskTitle ?? '';
  return (
    <Modal opened={workstream !== null} onClose={onClose} title="Open in terminal?">
      <Stack>
        <Text size="sm">
          {workstream?.state === WorkstreamState.Failed
            ? `"${title}" failed. 10db opens its conversation in a terminal here; when you hand back, 10db retries it from there.`
            : `10db pauses "${title}" at its next safe point, within a minute, and opens the conversation in a terminal here.`}
        </Text>
        <List size="sm" spacing={4}>
          <List.Item>
            10db&apos;s guard is off there. Commit with git as usual; the commits are credited to
            this task when you hand back.
          </List.Item>
          <List.Item>
            Other sessions may share this checkout: don&apos;t switch branches, stash or reset.
          </List.Item>
          <List.Item>
            Claude runs with its account&apos;s own Claude Code settings: on any account but the
            default, your ~/.claude settings and plugins aren&apos;t loaded.
          </List.Item>
          <List.Item>
            Leaving the terminal leaves Claude running. Quit Claude with /exit, then press Hand back.
          </List.Item>
        </List>
        {error ? (
          <Alert color="red" title="The daemon refused that">
            {error.message}
          </Alert>
        ) : null}
        <Group justify="flex-end">
          <Button variant="default" onClick={onClose}>
            Keep it as it is
          </Button>
          <Button loading={loading} onClick={onConfirm}>
            Open in terminal
          </Button>
        </Group>
      </Stack>
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/terminal/use-takeover.ts`:

```ts
import type { WorkstreamDto, WorkstreamId } from '@10db/contracts';
import { useNavigate } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { useState } from 'react';
import { useHandBack, useTakeOver } from '../../daemon/queries';
import { takeoverActions } from './takeover-actions';
import type { TakeoverModalProps } from './takeover-modal';

/** Takeover and hand-back for a view: a confirmation before the first takeover, none to reopen. */
export function useTakeoverFlow() {
  const takeOver = useTakeOver();
  const handBack = useHandBack();
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [confirming, setConfirming] = useState<WorkstreamDto | null>(null);
  const toTerminal = (id: WorkstreamId): void => {
    void navigate({ to: '/terminal/$workstreamId', params: { workstreamId: id } });
  };
  const close = (): void => {
    setConfirming(null);
    takeOver.reset();
  };
  const modal: TakeoverModalProps = {
    workstream: confirming,
    loading: takeOver.isPending,
    error: confirming ? takeOver.error : null,
    onClose: close,
    onConfirm: () => {
      if (confirming)
        takeOver.mutate(confirming.id, {
          onSuccess: (taken) => {
            close();
            toTerminal(taken.id);
          },
        });
    },
  };
  return {
    open(ws: WorkstreamDto): void {
      if (takeoverActions(ws).open === 'reopen')
        takeOver.mutate(ws.id, {
          onSuccess: (taken) => {
            toTerminal(taken.id);
          },
        });
      else setConfirming(ws);
    },
    show(ws: WorkstreamDto): void {
      toTerminal(ws.id);
    },
    handBack(ws: WorkstreamDto): void {
      handBack.mutate(ws.id);
    },
    busy: (id: WorkstreamId): boolean =>
      (takeOver.isPending && takeOver.variables === id) ||
      (handBack.isPending && handBack.variables === id),
    error: (confirming ? null : takeOver.error) ?? handBack.error,
    modal,
  };
}

export type TakeoverFlow = ReturnType<typeof useTakeoverFlow>;
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/terminal/takeover-buttons.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { WorkstreamDto } from '@10db/contracts';
import { Button } from '@mantine/core';
import { OPEN_LABELS, SHOW_TERMINAL, takeoverActions } from './takeover-actions';
import type { TakeoverFlow } from './use-takeover';

/** Open in terminal, Show terminal, Open terminal again and Hand back, as the workstream allows. */
export function TakeoverButtons({
  workstream,
  flow,
  inTerminal = false,
}: {
  readonly workstream: WorkstreamDto;
  readonly flow: TakeoverFlow;
  /** In the terminal view itself, which needs no Show terminal. */
  readonly inTerminal?: boolean;
}) {
  const actions = takeoverActions(workstream);
  const busy = flow.busy(workstream.id);
  return (
    <>
      {actions.show && !inTerminal ? (
        <Button
          size="xs"
          variant="light"
          onClick={() => {
            flow.show(workstream);
          }}
        >
          {SHOW_TERMINAL}
        </Button>
      ) : null}
      {actions.open ? (
        <Button
          size="xs"
          variant="light"
          loading={busy}
          onClick={() => {
            flow.open(workstream);
          }}
        >
          {OPEN_LABELS[actions.open]}
        </Button>
      ) : null}
      {actions.handBack ? (
        <Button
          size="xs"
          disabled={actions.handBack === 'disabled'}
          loading={busy && actions.handBack === 'enabled'}
          onClick={() => {
            flow.handBack(workstream);
          }}
        >
          Hand back
        </Button>
      ) : null}
    </>
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/terminal/xterm-screen.ts`:

```ts
import '@xterm/xterm/css/xterm.css';
import { TERMINAL_SIZE_LIMITS, type TerminalSize } from '@10db/contracts';
import { FitAddon } from '@xterm/addon-fit';
import { Terminal } from '@xterm/xterm';
import type { TerminalScreen } from './terminal-screen';

const SCROLLBACK_LINES = 5_000;

/** The size the PTY accepts, whatever the fit computed for a tiny or a huge pane. */
function bounded({ cols, rows }: TerminalSize): TerminalSize {
  const { minCols, maxCols, minRows, maxRows } = TERMINAL_SIZE_LIMITS;
  return {
    cols: Math.min(maxCols, Math.max(minCols, cols)),
    rows: Math.min(maxRows, Math.max(minRows, rows)),
  };
}

/** xterm.js in `element`, fitted to it as it resizes, in the theme's monospace font. */
export function createXtermScreen(element: HTMLElement): TerminalScreen {
  const fontFamily = getComputedStyle(document.documentElement)
    .getPropertyValue('--mantine-font-family-monospace')
    .trim();
  const terminal = new Terminal({
    cursorBlink: true,
    scrollback: SCROLLBACK_LINES,
    ...(fontFamily ? { fontFamily } : {}),
  });
  const fit = new FitAddon();
  terminal.loadAddon(fit);
  terminal.open(element);
  fit.fit();
  const observer = new ResizeObserver(() => {
    fit.fit();
  });
  observer.observe(element);
  return {
    write(data) {
      terminal.write(data);
    },
    onInput(listener) {
      const subscription = terminal.onData(listener);
      return () => {
        subscription.dispose();
      };
    },
    onResize(listener) {
      const subscription = terminal.onResize((size) => {
        listener(bounded(size));
      });
      return () => {
        subscription.dispose();
      };
    },
    size: () => bounded({ cols: terminal.cols, rows: terminal.rows }),
    focus() {
      terminal.focus();
    },
    dispose() {
      observer.disconnect();
      terminal.dispose();
    },
  };
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/terminal/terminal-channel.ts`:

```ts
import {
  TERMINAL_INPUT_MAX,
  TerminalCloseCode,
  TerminalServerMessage,
  type TerminalClientMessage,
  type WorkstreamId,
} from '@10db/contracts';
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import type { DaemonConnection } from '../../daemon/connection';
import { useDaemon } from '../../daemon/provider';
import type { TerminalScreen } from './terminal-screen';

export const TerminalChannelState = {
  Connecting: 'connecting',
  Attached: 'attached',
  Ended: 'ended',
  NoTerminal: 'no_terminal',
  Closed: 'closed',
} as const;
export type TerminalChannelState = (typeof TerminalChannelState)[keyof typeof TerminalChannelState];

/** The terminal socket takes the token in the query, as the event stream does: a browser sets no header on it. */
export function terminalSocketUrl(conn: DaemonConnection, id: WorkstreamId): string {
  const url = new URL(`/workstreams/${id}/terminal`, conn.url);
  url.protocol = url.protocol === 'https:' ? 'wss:' : 'ws:';
  url.searchParams.set('token', conn.token);
  return url.toString();
}

/** `text` in pieces the daemon accepts, never splitting a surrogate pair. */
function pieces(text: string): string[] {
  const out: string[] = [];
  let start = 0;
  while (start < text.length) {
    let end = Math.min(start + TERMINAL_INPUT_MAX, text.length);
    const last = text.charCodeAt(end - 1);
    if (end < text.length && last >= 0xd800 && last <= 0xdbff) end -= 1;
    out.push(text.slice(start, end));
    start = end;
  }
  return out;
}

function parse(data: unknown): TerminalServerMessage | null {
  if (typeof data !== 'string') return null;
  try {
    const parsed = TerminalServerMessage.safeParse(JSON.parse(data));
    return parsed.success ? parsed.data : null;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

/** Connects a screen to the workstream's terminal: keystrokes and sizes out, output in (spec §5.4). */
export function useTerminalChannel(
  id: WorkstreamId,
  screen: TerminalScreen | null,
): TerminalChannelState {
  const { connection } = useDaemon();
  const [state, setState] = useState<TerminalChannelState>(TerminalChannelState.Connecting);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!screen) return;
    const socket = new WebSocket(terminalSocketUrl(connection, id));
    const send = (message: TerminalClientMessage): void => {
      if (socket.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN) socket.send(JSON.stringify(message));
    };
    socket.addEventListener('open', () => {
      setState(TerminalChannelState.Attached);
      send({ type: 'resize', ...screen.size() });
    });
    socket.addEventListener('message', (event) => {
      const message = parse(event.data);
      if (!message) return;
      switch (message.type) {
        case 'output':
          screen.write(message.data);
          return;
        case 'exit':
          // The output already says how it ended; the view's note follows the workstream.
          setState(TerminalChannelState.Ended);
          return;
      }
    });
    socket.addEventListener('close', (event) => {
      setState(
        event.code === TerminalCloseCode.NoTerminal
          ? TerminalChannelState.NoTerminal
          : TerminalChannelState.Closed,
      );
    });
    const stopInput = screen.onInput((data) => {
      for (const piece of pieces(data)) send({ type: 'input', data: piece });
    });
    const stopResize = screen.onResize((size) => {
      send({ type: 'resize', ...size });
    });
    return () => {
      stopInput();
      stopResize();
      socket.close();
    };
  }, [connection, id, screen]);
  return state;
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/terminal/terminal-pane.tsx`:

```tsx
import type { WorkstreamId } from '@10db/contracts';
import { Box, Stack, Text } from '@mantine/core';
import { use, useCallback, useState } from 'react';
import { TerminalChannelState, useTerminalChannel } from './terminal-channel';
import { ScreenFactoryContext, type TerminalScreen } from './terminal-screen';

/** The terminal itself: a screen on the daemon's PTY. Leaving it ends nothing (spec §5.4). */
export function TerminalPane({ workstreamId }: { readonly workstreamId: WorkstreamId }) {
  const createScreen = use(ScreenFactoryContext);
  const [screen, setScreen] = useState<TerminalScreen | null>(null);
  const state = useTerminalChannel(workstreamId, screen);
  // A ref callback with a new identity runs the old one's cleanup and then itself, which would
  // dispose xterm, reopen the socket and replay the scrollback on every render. Its identity is
  // what decides that, so it is held stable here whatever the compiler does with it.
  const attach = useCallback(
    (element: HTMLDivElement | null) => {
      if (!element) return;
      const created = createScreen(element);
      setScreen(created);
      created.focus();
      return () => {
        created.dispose();
      };
    },
    [createScreen],
  );
  return (
    <Stack gap="xs">
      <Box
        aria-label="Terminal"
        role="region"
        h="65vh"
        mih={320}
        ref={attach}
      />
      {state === TerminalChannelState.NoTerminal ? (
        <Text size="sm" c="dimmed">
          10db kept no output for this terminal: it ended when 10db stopped.
        </Text>
      ) : null}
    </Stack>
  );
}
```

`apps/desktop/src/views/terminal/terminal-view.tsx`:

```tsx
import { WorkstreamState, type WorkstreamId } from '@10db/contracts';
import { Alert, Button, Group, Loader, Stack, Text, Title } from '@mantine/core';
import { Link } from '@tanstack/react-router';
import { useWorkstreams } from '../../daemon/queries';
import { StateBadge } from '../factory/state-badge';
import { takeoverActions } from './takeover-actions';
import { TakeoverButtons } from './takeover-buttons';
import { TakeoverModal } from './takeover-modal';
import { TerminalPane } from './terminal-pane';
import { useTakeoverFlow } from './use-takeover';

/** A taken-over workstream's terminal, inside the 10db window (spec §5.4, §10). */
export function TerminalView({ workstreamId }: { readonly workstreamId: WorkstreamId }) {
  const workstreams = useWorkstreams({ active: false });
  const flow = useTakeoverFlow();
  const ws = workstreams.data?.find((w) => w.id === workstreamId);
  if (workstreams.isPending) return <Loader size="sm" aria-label="Loading the workstream" />;
  if (!ws) return <Text c="dimmed">No such workstream.</Text>;
  const note = takeoverActions(ws).note;
  return (
    <Stack>
      <Group justify="space-between">
        <Title order={2}>{`Terminal: ${ws.taskTitle}`}</Title>
        <Group gap="xs">
          <TakeoverButtons workstream={ws} flow={flow} inTerminal />
          <Button component={Link} to="/factory" size="xs" variant="default">
            Back to the Factory
          </Button>
        </Group>
      </Group>
      <Group gap="xs">
        <StateBadge state={ws.state} />
        {note ? (
          <Text size="sm" c="dimmed">
            {note}
          </Text>
        ) : null}
      </Group>
      {flow.error ? (
        <Alert color="red" title="The daemon refused that">
          {flow.error.message}
        </Alert>
      ) : null}
      {ws.state === WorkstreamState.TakenOver && ws.takeover ? (
        // A new terminal, or the end of one, attaches a fresh socket to what the daemon kept.
        <TerminalPane key={ws.takeover.terminalSessionId ?? 'closed'} workstreamId={ws.id} />
      ) : (
        <Text size="sm" c="dimmed">{`"${ws.taskTitle}" is ${ws.state}; it has no terminal now.`}</Text>
      )}
      <TakeoverModal {...flow.modal} />
    </Stack>
  );
}
```

```ts
const TAKEN = 'Take me over';

test('takes a running task over in the terminal, types in it, exits and hands back', async ({ page }) => {
  await page.goto('/board');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'New task' }).click();
  const taskDialog = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'New task' });
  await taskDialog.getByLabel('Title').fill(TAKEN);
  await taskDialog.getByLabel('Acceptance criteria').fill('the builder looked at it');
  await taskDialog.getByRole('button', { name: 'Create task' }).click();
  await expect(taskDialog).toBeHidden();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: `Actions for ${TAKEN}` }).click();
  await page.getByRole('menuitem', { name: 'Start' }).click();

  await open(page, 'Factory');
  const row = page.getByRole('row', { name: new RegExp(TAKEN) });
  await expect(row.getByText('Running', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
  await row.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open in terminal' }).click();
  const confirm = page.getByRole('dialog', { name: 'Open in terminal?' });
  await expect(confirm.getByText(/don't switch branches, stash or reset/)).toBeVisible();
  await confirm.getByRole('button', { name: 'Open in terminal' }).click();

  // The terminal view, inside the window: the daemon's PTY runs the fake claude.
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: `Terminal: ${TAKEN}`, level: 2 })).toBeVisible();
  const terminal = page.getByRole('region', { name: 'Terminal' });
  await expect(terminal).toContainText('fake claude: ready');
  await expect(terminal).toContainText(`you are driving "${TAKEN}"`);
  await expect(page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hand back' })).toBeDisabled();
  await expectAccessible(page, 'terminal, dark');

  await terminal.click();
  await page.keyboard.type('hello from the builder');
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  await expect(terminal).toContainText('you said: hello from the builder');

  // Leaving the view leaves Claude running; coming back shows what it printed.
  await open(page, 'Factory');
  await expect(row.getByText(/Claude is running in the terminal/)).toBeVisible();
  await row.getByRole('button', { name: 'Show terminal' }).click();
  await expect(terminal).toContainText('you said: hello from the builder');

  await terminal.click();
  await page.keyboard.type('/exit');
  await page.keyboard.press('Enter');
  await expect(terminal).toContainText('fake claude: bye');
  await expect(page.getByText('Claude exited with status 0. Hand back when you are done.')).toBeVisible();
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Hand back' }).click();

  await open(page, 'Factory');
  await page.getByRole('switch', { name: 'Show finished' }).check();
  await expect(row.getByText('Done', { exact: true })).toBeVisible();
});
```

`expectAccessible` scans the whole page. xterm.js draws its own grid, and its accessibility is its own (it has a screen-reader mode); exclude it from the scan with `new AxeBuilder({ page }).exclude('.xterm')` when the label starts with `terminal`, and keep the scan of the view around it.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the architecture section**

`docs/architecture/04-takeover.md` (`tests/repo/src/architecture-docs.test.ts` checks the three header lines; the accounts plan took `03`; if another section took `04` by now, take the next number):

```markdown
# 04 Takeover

- **Status:** Locked
- **Scope:** "Open in terminal": the builder takes a managed or failed Claude session over in a terminal inside the 10db window, then hands it back.
- **Summary:** The design is spec §5.4 of `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-10db-factory-loop-design.md` and plan 10; its decisions are rows <first>–<last> of `decisions.md`. Changes are new rows that name what they supersede.

## Where it lives

| Module                                                 | Job                                                                                                      |
| ------------------------------------------------------ | -------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------------- |
| `packages/contracts` `takeover.ts`                     | `TAKEABLE_STATES`, the takeover record and DTO, the terminal channel's messages                          |
| `packages/db` `workstreams.takeover`                   | the record while a terminal owns the conversation; `enterTakeover`                                       |
| `apps/daemon/src/runner/`                              | `Runner.takeOver`: the safe point (next tool call denied, end of turn, a wait, or `takeoverWaitMs`)      |
| `apps/daemon/src/terminal/`                            | the PTYs (`@lydell/node-pty`), each terminal's bounded scrollback, its panes, the shutdown               |
| `apps/daemon/src/takeover/`                            | takeover, the terminal's launch through the accounts launcher, rescue, hand-back and cancel              |
| `apps/daemon/src/api/terminal-channel.ts`              | the WebSocket a pane types through, with the event stream's auth                                         |
| `apps/daemon/src/watcher/`, `keep-awake/`, `recovery/` | a taken-over session is watched, keeps the Mac awake while its terminal runs, and stays taken over across a restart |
| `apps/desktop/src/views/terminal/`                     | the terminal view (xterm.js), Open in terminal, Show terminal, Open terminal again and Hand back         |

## Lifecycle

RUNNING | HANDING_OFF | WAITING_HUMAN | FAILED → TAKEN_OVER → (hand back) RECOVERING → QUEUED with `resume` on the same conversation; TAKEN_OVER → CANCELLED. A terminal is open while its process runs and closed once it exits or the daemon stops; a closed one can be opened again. A rescued (once FAILED) workstream's hand-back also resets its failure count and reopens a failed batch, as Retry does.

## Invariants

- A conversation has one driver: hand back, cancel and opening again are refused while Claude runs in the terminal, checked again inside each one's write. Two panes on one terminal are one driver.
- One workstream per project is taken over at a time.
- A takeover never waits on the gate: it is refused while the gate runs, and one with nothing to take over lapses at once.
- The daemon owns the terminal's process: closing a pane or the window ends nothing; a shutdown or restart ends it and records why, and the workstream stays taken over.
- The terminal runs on the project's current account, in the environment the accounts launcher builds; no other code names an account variable. It resumes the conversation when it ran on that account, and otherwise starts a fresh one with a known id, briefed from the step's handoff, which hand-back then resumes.
- On a subscription account a terminal takes the account's start gate from spawn until Claude first prints or exits, at most 30 s.
- Terminal output stays in memory, bounded per terminal; it never reaches disk, a log, an event or the database.
- The terminal channel has the event stream's auth: the UI token, the allowed origins, loopback only.
- A terminal session is not guarded; its commits are credited at hand-back unless another workstream recorded them, and stray paths are flagged, never refused.
- Hand back needs the takeover HEAD in history and the right branch: the batch branch for work in an unfinished batch, else the branch at takeover.
- A taken-over workstream holds its claims, no lease, no lane; it blocks shipping and abandoning its batch.
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Document takeover in the README**

In `README.md`, after the "Deliver through pull requests" section the batch plan added (and the accounts section the accounts plan added), add:

```markdown
## Take over a session in a terminal

**Open in terminal** (in the Factory, or a task card's menu) pauses a running or waiting session at its next
safe point (within a minute) and opens the same conversation with `claude --resume` in a terminal inside the
10db window, in the project's folder and on the project's account. If the project has moved to another account
since the conversation ran, the terminal starts a fresh conversation there instead, briefed from the task's latest
handoff, and the banner says so. A failed task can be opened the same way: when you hand it back, 10db retries it
from there. While you drive it:

- 10db's guard is off: commit with git as usual. When you hand back, 10db credits those commits to the task and
  flags any file outside its areas.
- Other sessions may share the checkout: don't switch branches, stash or reset. Hand back is refused until the
  checkout is back on the branch it should be on.
- On any account but the default one, Claude runs in that account's own Claude Code config directory, so your
  `~/.claude` settings and plugins aren't there.
- Leaving the terminal view, or closing the window, leaves Claude running; **Show terminal** brings it back with
  its recent output. Quitting 10db ends the terminal; **Open terminal again** resumes the conversation.
- Don't resume the same conversation in a tab of your own: 10db only knows about the terminal it opened.
- One session per project can be taken over at a time; hand it back (or cancel it) before you take over another.
- A session whose gate is running can't be taken over; wait for the gate to finish.

Quit Claude with `/exit`, then press **Hand back**: 10db checks git and resumes the conversation in managed mode.
```

```
- `terminal/` owns the daemon's PTYs: it spawns a program under a key, keeps its newest output
  (`scrollbackChars`) in memory only, streams it to attached panes, and ends every terminal at
  shutdown (SIGHUP, then SIGKILL). Only `terminal/pty.ts` imports `@lydell/node-pty`. Tests run the
  real PTY with the fake `claude` in `test/fake-claude.ts`.
- `takeover/` owns "Open in terminal" (spec §5.4). The runner pauses the session at a safe point
  (`Runner.takeOver`); the service spawns `claude` in a terminal with the accounts launcher's
  environment for the project's account (`--resume`, or a fresh `--session-id` conversation briefed
  from the handoff when the project left the old one's account), takes the subscription start gate
  until Claude first prints, rescues failed work, and hands back after
  `handBackRefusal`. A terminal session (`mode: terminal`) is human-driven and unguarded. Opening a
  terminal, hand-back and cancel re-read the workstream inside their write (`stillTakenOver`).
- `api/terminal-channel.ts` is the pane's WebSocket (`/workstreams/:id/terminal`). It has the event
  stream's auth, parses every message with `TerminalClientMessage`, and closes on a bad one; the
  server refuses any frame over `TERMINAL_FRAME_MAX_BYTES`.
- A signed release signs the bundle's native files itself (`scripts/release/sign-native.sh`, Tauri's
  `beforeBundleCommand`); a new native file in the bundle goes on its list.
```

## Self-review notes

- **Spec coverage:**
  - Decision 3 (explicit, serialized takeover; never two drivers): `TAKEABLE_STATES` and the safe point (Tasks 1, 5); `refuseWhileRunning` on reopen, hand back and cancel, and `stillTakenOver` re-checking inside each one's write (Tasks 6, 8); one takeover per project (T11, Task 6).
  - §3 "The PTY lives in the Tauri shell": amended by T1; the PTY lives in the daemon (Task 4), wired in Task 11.
  - §5.1 `TAKEN_OVER`: the state existed; the edges from `HANDING_OFF`, `WAITING_HUMAN` and `FAILED` (Task 1), the record (Task 2).
  - §5.4 Takeover:
    - step 1, pause at a safe point: Task 5 (with the lapse when there is nothing to take over), and Task 6's refusal while the gate runs;
    - step 2, release the lease and `claude --resume <id>` in the PTY owns the session: Tasks 2 (`enterTakeover`), 4 (the PTY host) and 6 (the terminal's launch);
    - step 3, the builder clicks Hand back once the terminal exits: Tasks 8, 11, 12 and 13;
    - step 4, reconcile git: Task 8 (branch, history, credited commits, claims) and Task 9 (batch branch);
    - step 5, managed mode resumes: Task 8 (`resume` on the same conversation; the existing resume prompt carries the reason), and Task 10 for a rescued workstream.
  - §9 `SessionStart` injection for terminal sessions: amended by T2.
  - §10 _Open in terminal_ and xterm.js: the Factory has no detail view yet, so the row and the board card open the terminal view (Tasks 12, 13; T1); xterm.js draws it (Task 12).
  - §13 M2 takeover: this plan.
  - The interactions the brief names: takeover while `SHIPPING` (Task 9); git reconcile against claims and the batch branch, including a terminal that switched branches (Tasks 8, 9); a crash, a restart and a shutdown while `TAKEN_OVER` (Task 9); the guard does not apply to the terminal, and commits are attributed at hand-back (T2, T7, T11; Task 8); keep-awake (Task 7); the accounts launcher and a conversation on another account (Task 6); a failed workstream rescued, in and out of a batch (Task 10).
- **Readings of the spec this plan relies on** (each is a decision row or still open for Travis): the PTY lives in the daemon and the pane is xterm.js in the window (T1); the terminal session gets no harness and no `SessionStart` injection, only a system-prompt note, and its environment is the accounts launcher's (T2); "a safe point" is the next tool call, the end of the turn or a wait, with a 60 s interrupt, and never the gate (T3); a taken-over session holds no lane (T4); a shutdown or restart ends the terminal and keeps the takeover (T10); hand-back off the branch is refused rather than failing the batch (T6); one takeover per project (T11); a failed workstream can be rescued (T12); the terminal runs on the project's current account, starting a fresh conversation from the handoff when the old one lives on an account the project left (T2, Travis's decision).
- **Review Focus coverage:** each of the eleven items names the test that pins it; every one of those tests is in a task's Step 1 (or, for Task 9's shutdown case, beside its recovery case).
- **The plan review** (Fable, on the revision at `30f5790`) found no Critical issue; this revision applies its findings: a stable ref callback for the pane, signing of the native files, no new terminal after shutdown begins, a frame limit on the WebSocket server, the exit signal, Retry's answer on a rescued card, the cut refused under a takeover, Retry's checks inside the rescue's writes, no wall-clock assertion, a group SIGKILL, and the pid-recording limit stated; and Travis's four decisions (a fresh conversation after a move, the start gate, unmetered spend, the card left open).
- **Each task stands alone:** Task 4's host knows no workstream and is tested on its own; Task 5's runner is complete without the service (its lapse and gate rule are tested through `Runner.takeOver`); Task 6's `stillTakenOver` already guards `open()` before Task 8 reuses it; Task 7's watcher rule reads only `state`; Task 10 builds on Tasks 6, 8 and 9 without changing their tests; Task 11 is the first to bundle the PTY, so the bundle test goes red there; Task 13's new props are optional, so Task 12's tree builds before it.
- **Types used across tasks:**
  - `TakeoverRecord` (with `accountId`, `rescued`, `freshFrom`, `lastExit`), `TakeoverDto`, `TerminalStatus`, `TerminalEndReason`, `TerminalExit` (with `signal`), `TAKEABLE_STATES`, `TerminalSize`, `TERMINAL_SIZE_LIMITS`, `TERMINAL_INPUT_MAX`, `TERMINAL_FRAME_MAX_BYTES`, `TerminalCloseCode`, `TerminalClientMessage`, `TerminalServerMessage`, `EventType.TakeoverChanged`: Task 1; used in 2 and 4–14.
  - `enterTakeover`, `setSessionProcess`, `latestConversation`, `commitShasRecordedAfter`, `WorkstreamDto.takeover`: Task 2; used in 5–10.
  - `Git.isAncestor`, `Git.commitFiles`: Task 3; used in 8.
  - `createTerminalHost`, `TerminalHost`, `TerminalSpec` (with `onFirstOutput`), `TerminalEnd`, `TerminalViewer`, `TerminalAttachment`, `TerminalSpawnError`, `DEFAULT_TERMINAL_LIMITS`, `nodePtySpawner`, `createScrollback`, `createFakeClaude`: Task 4; used in 6, 8, 9, 11.
  - `Runner.takeOver` (with its lapse), `StopReason 'takeover'`, `RunnerLimits.takeoverWaitMs`, `TAKEOVER_REFUSAL`, `TAKEOVER_NOTICE`, `cancelWorkstreamCards`: Task 5; used in 6, 8, 11.

- **Assumptions to check while executing:**
  - The accounts plan's names (`Launcher.session`, `AccountLaunchError`, `SessionLaunch`, `unready`, `AccountRuntime.blocked` and `refused`, `getAccountRow`, `seedAccount`, `patchAccount`, `AccountRow.archived`, `AccountRow.configDir`, `SessionRow.accountId`, `TestEngine.accounts`, `TestEngine.keychain`, `createMemoryKeychain`, and the names `start.ts` gives the launcher and the account runtime) (and `getSessionByProviderId`, `patchProject`, `StartGate` with `TestEngine.startGate`, `capCard`, `latestHandoffForStep`'s use for a moved lane, `briefedFromHandoff` and `nextActionOnAnotherAccount`) are taken from its plan text at `86e0e67`. Where the merged code differs, trust the code and say so.
  - The batch plan's names (`createDeliveryHarness` and its `starter`, `shipper`, `jobs`, `settle`, `git$`; `recoverBatches`; `delivery/ship.ts`'s `abandon` and `shipAnyway`) were read from `main` at `bee3f2e`.
  - `claude --resume <id>` keeps the session id (`--fork-session` is off by default, per `claude --help` on 2.1.306), finds the transcript in the account's `CLAUDE_CONFIG_DIR`, and applies `--append-system-prompt` to the resumed conversation. `claude --session-id <uuid> … <prompt>` starts an interactive conversation with that id and sends the prompt first: `--session-id` ("Use a specific session ID for the conversation (must be a valid UUID)") and the positional `[prompt]` are in `claude --help` on Claude Code 2.1.309, read on 2026-10-23. Task 16 checks all of them live.
  - `@lydell/node-pty` 1.1.0 spawned, resized and echoed through a PTY on Node 26.12.0, darwin-arm64, on 2026-10-23; the CI gate's Linux image runs its `linux-x64` package, and the first CI run of Task 4 checks it.
  - A missing or unrunnable program in a node-pty spawn doesn't throw: its child exits with status 1 (checked on darwin-arm64). That is why `terminalStart` checks `claude` with `accessSync` first.
  - xterm.js 6 needs no change to the Tauri CSP: it draws with inline styles, which `style-src 'unsafe-inline'` already allows, and the socket is `ws://127.0.0.1:*`, which `connect-src` already allows.
  - `git diff-tree --diff-merges=first-parent` needs git 2.31 or later; macOS ships 2.50 and the CI image 2.43.
  - Task 5's no-conversation lapse test relies on the first session reading `HEAD` (`baseHead` is null) before it starts, so a `takeOver` in the launch's tick lands before any session exists. If the code reads it elsewhere, start the takeover from the same point another way and say so.
